import * as React from 'react';
import { Size, Alignment } from 'core/enums';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { IFormInputOption } from 'core/interfaces';
import { IRadioGroupProps } from './types';
import { SCRadioGroup, SCRadioOption, SCRadioChildren, SCLabelDescription } from './styles';
import { IRadioGroupProps } from './types';
import { SCRadioGroup, SCRadioOption, SCRadioChildren, SCLabelDescription } from './styles';
const getAriaLabel = (element: IFormInputOption) => {  
if (element.ariaLabel) return element.ariaLabel;  
if (typeof element.label === 'string') return element.label;  
return undefined;
};
/** 
* Accepts an array of options that are mapped out as individual `RadioButton` components. 
*/
const RadioGroup = ({  
name,  
value,  
options,  
alignment = Alignment.HORIZONTAL,  
displayMode = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
radioSize = Size.DEFAULT,  
showVerticalDivider = true,  
onChange = (): void => {},  
groupLabelledBy,  
error = false,  
disabled = false,  
className,  
radioGroupVariant = undefined,  
...rest
}: IRadioGroupProps): JSX.Element => {  
return (    
<SCRadioGroup      
role='radiogroup'      
aria-labelledby={groupLabelledBy}      
alignment={alignment}      
colorScheme={displayMode}      
error={error}      
variant={radioGroupVariant}      
{...rest}>      
{options.map((element, i) => (        
<SCRadioOption          
name={name}          
id={`${name}-option-${i + 1}-${element.value}`}          
alignment={alignment}          
checked={value === element.value}          
value={element.value}          
key={element.value}          
size={radioSize}          
colorScheme={displayMode}          
onChange={onChange}          
ariaLabel={getAriaLabel(element)}          
showVerticalDivider={showVerticalDivider}          
error={error}          
disabled={disabled || element.disabled}          
className={`${className} CUNARadioOption`}          
hasLabelDescription={!!element.labelDescription}>          
{element.labelDescription ? (            
<SCRadioChildren>              
{element.label}              
<SCLabelDescription disabled={disabled || element.disabled}>                
{element.labelDescription}              
</SCLabelDescription>            
</SCRadioChildren>          
) : (            
element.label          
)}        
</SCRadioOption>      
))}    
</SCRadioGroup>  
);
};
export { RadioGroup };
```

/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon RangeStepperInput value change.   
*/  
onChange?: (name: string, value: string) => void;  
/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon RangeStepperInput blur.   
*/  
onBlur?: (name: string, value: string) => void;  
/**   
* Optional prefix preceding the value in the RangeStepperInput.   
*/  
prefix?: JSX.Element | string;  
/**   
* Boolean indicating if there's an error with the RangeStepperInput value.   
*/  
hasError?: boolean;  
/**   
* Name attribute to get the input.   
*/  
name: string;  
/**   
* Boolean indicating if the RangeStepperInput is read-only.   
* Wrapper for `StepperInputView` component. Defines and passes input handlers to `StepperInputView`. 
*/
const StepperInput = ({  
id,  
value,  
formatter,  
interval = 1,  
min = 0,  
max = Number.MAX_VALUE,  
labelledBy,  
onChange = (): void => {},  
onBlur = (): void => {},  
placeholder,  
...props
}: IStepperInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const getValueInBoundaries = useCallback((value: number): number => Math.max(Math.min(value, max), min), [min, max]);
  const [isFocused, setFocused] = useState(false);  
const exitFocusMode = useCallback((): void => {    
setFocused(false);  
}, [setFocused]);  
const enterFocusMode = useCallback((): void => {    
setFocused(true);  
}, [setFocused]);
  const getOutValue = useCallback(    
setFocused(false);  
}, [setFocused]);  
const enterFocusMode = useCallback((): void => {    
setFocused(true);  
}, [setFocused]);
  const getOutValue = useCallback(    
(stringValue: string): number | undefined => {      
const value = formatter ? formatter.out(stringValue) : parseInt(stringValue, 10);
      if (!value && value !== 0) return undefined;
      return getValueInBoundaries(value);    
},    
[getValueInBoundaries, formatter]  
);
  const onChangeHandler = useCallback(    
(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => onChange(getOutValue(e.target.value)),    
[onChange, getOutValue]  
);
  const onBlurHandler = useCallback(    
(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
onBlur(getOutValue(e.target.value));      
exitFocusMode();    
},    
[onBlur, getOutValue, exitFocusMode]  
);
/>  
);
};
export { StepperInput };
```

```typescript
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { StyledComponent } from 'styled-components';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles/utility';
import { ITextAreaProps } from './types';
import { SCTextAreaWrapper } from './styles';
/** 
* 
* @summary 
* Basic Textarea component 
* It display depend on display mode prop and also changing theme depend on state of input. 
*/
const TextArea = ({  
id,  
name,  
onChange,  
onBlur,  
disabled,  
disabled,  
placeholder,  
className,  
value,  
label,  
displayMode,  
isDynamic,
}: ITextAreaProps): JSX.Element => {  
const ref = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {    
if (ref.current && isDynamic) {      
ref.current.style.height = 'auto';      
const scrollheight = ref.current.scrollHeight;      
ref.current.style.height = `${scrollheight}px`;    
}  
}, [value]);  
// Typescript has trouble understanding the "as" prop in styled components.  
const TextArea = SCUtilityInput as StyledComponent<'textarea', any, {}, never>;  
return (    
<SCTextAreaWrapper colorScheme={displayMode} isDynamic={isDynamic}>      
<TextArea        
ref={ref}        
as='textarea'        
id={id}        
name={name}        
onChange={onChange}        
onBlur={onBlur}        
className={className}        
disabled={disabled}        
placeholder={placeholder}        
aria-invalid        
value={value}        
aria-label={label}        
rows={isDynamic ? 1 : 2}      
/>    
</SCTextAreaWrapper>  
);
};
export { TextArea };
```

data-testid={buttonId}            
onClick={onClose}            
ref={buttonRef}>            
<img alt='close growl' src={closeIcon} />          
</Button>        
</SCGrowlButtonWrapper>      
)}    
</SCGrowlWrapper>  
);
};
```

...Object.fromEntries([Object.keys(ICON_TYPES)].map((iconType) => [iconType, null])),  
[ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_DOWN]: SCArrowDown,  
[ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_UP]: SCArrowUp,
};
/** 
* Base level SVG icon component. 
*/
const IconComponent = ({  
className,  
name,  
size,  
ariaLabel,  
focusable = false,  
id,  
...props
}: IIconProps): JSX.Element => {  
let GenericIcon: JSX.Element;  
const Icon = nameToFile[name];  
if (Icon !== null) {    
GenericIcon = (      
<Icon        
className={className}        
aria-label={ariaLabel}        
aria-hidden={!ariaLabel}        
role='img'        
focusable={focusable}        
{...(size && { width: size, height: size })}        
style={{ display: 'block' }}        
{...props}      
/>    
);  
} else {    
const Icon = nameToStyledComponent[name] ?? null;    
GenericIcon = <Icon className={className} size={size} />;  
}  
return (    
<SCIcon className={className} id={id} {...(Icon === null ? props : {})}>      
{GenericIcon}    
focusable={focusable}        
{...(size && { width: size, height: size })}        
style={{ display: 'block' }}        
{...props}      
/>    
);  
} else {    
const Icon = nameToStyledComponent[name] ?? null;    
GenericIcon = <Icon className={className} size={size} />;  
}  
return (    
<SCIcon className={className} id={id} {...(Icon === null ? props : {})}>      
{GenericIcon}    
</SCIcon>  
);
};
export { IconComponent };

import { IImageComponentProps } from './types';
export const FALLBACK_SRC =  
'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAMAAAACCAQAAAA3fa6RAAAADklEQVR42mNkAANGCAUAACMAA2w/AMgAAAAASUVORK5CYII';
/** 
* Basic image component. Displays image if loaded and accepts fallback image. 
*/
const ImageComponent = ({ src, fallbackLabel, fallbackSrc, ...props }: IImageComponentProps): JSX.Element => {  
const [srcIsValid, setSrcIsValid] = useState<boolean>(!!src);
  const handleError = (): void => {    
setSrcIsValid(false);  
};
  const imgSrc = srcIsValid ? src : fallbackSrc || FALLBACK_SRC;
  return (    
<>      
<SCImg src={imgSrc} onError={handleError} {...props} />
      {!srcIsValid && fallbackLabel}    
</>  
);
};
export { ImageComponent };
```

import React, { ReactFragment } from 'react';
import './lineItem.css';
interface ILineItem {  
label: string;  
value?: ReactFragment;  
showDots?: boolean;  
section?: string;  
idx?: string;
}
/** 
* Renders a label value pair with dots rendered in between. 
* @param {string} label - The label will appear on the left side 
* @param {ReactFragment} value? - The value will appear on the right side. Defaults to empty string. 
* @param {boolean} showDots? - Defaults to true. 
* @param {string} section - The value for the id tag 
* @param {string} idx - The value for the id tag post fix 
*/
export const LineItem = ({ label, showDots = true, value = '', section = '', idx = '' }: ILineItem): JSX.Element => {  
const idPostFix = idx !== '' ? `_${idx}` : '';
const idPostFix = idx !== '' ? `_${idx}` : '';
  return (    
<div className='lineItem'>      
<div className='lineItemLabel' id={encodeURI(`${section}${label}Label${idPostFix}`)}>        
{label}      
</div>      
{showDots && <div className='lineDots' aria-hidden='true' />}      
<div className='lineItemValue' id={encodeURI(`${section}${label}Value${idPostFix}`)}>        
{value}      
</div>    
</div>  
);
};

import React from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { DLXLoanStatus } from 'core/enums';
import { LoanApplicationSubTypes as LoanSubType } from 'core/entities/loanApplication';
import { DottedProgressBar } from 'components/atoms/DottedProgressBar';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { ILoanProgressBarProps } from './types';
import { SCContainer } from './styles';
const LoanProgressBar = ({ loanAppId, dlxLoanStatus, subtype, forceDark }: ILoanProgressBarProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
  /**   
* Conditional screen reader description text based on loan type,   
* with dynamically updated states via mark-up params   
* */  
const defaultAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_DEFAULT');  
let applyAlt = defaultAlt;  
let reviewAlt = defaultAlt;  
let decisionAlt = defaultAlt;  
let fundingAlt = defaultAlt;  
switch (dlxLoanStatus) {    
case DLXLoanStatus.IDLE:    
case DLXLoanStatus.IN_PROGRESS:    
case DLXLoanStatus.TAKEN_OVER_BY_LOAN_OFFICER:      
applyAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_INCOMPLETE');      
break;    
case DLXLoanStatus.PENDING_DECISION:    
case DLXLoanStatus.UNDER_REVIEW:    
case DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED_PENDING:    
case DLXLoanStatus.ABANDONED:    
case DLXLoanStatus.NOT_ACTIVE:      
applyAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
reviewAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_INCOMPLETE');      
break;    
case DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED:    
case DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED_UPDATED:    
case DLXLoanStatus.DECLINED:    
case DLXLoanStatus.EXPIRED:      
applyAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
reviewAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
decisionAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
break;    
case DLXLoanStatus.FUNDED:      
applyAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
reviewAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
decisionAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
fundingAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
decisionAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
fundingAlt = t('LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_COMPLETE');      
break;    
case DLXLoanStatus.RESET:      
break;    
default:      
break;  
}
  const labelTemplate =    
subtype === LoanSubType.CREDIT_CARD || subtype === LoanSubType.PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT      
? 'LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_TRACKER_CC'      
: 'LOAN_STATUS_PANEL__PROGRESS_TRACKER';
  const filledDotsMap: { [key in DLXLoanStatus]?: number } = {    
[DLXLoanStatus.PENDING_DECISION]: 2,    
[DLXLoanStatus.UNDER_REVIEW]: 2,    
[DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED_PENDING]: 2,    
[DLXLoanStatus.ABANDONED]: 2,    
[DLXLoanStatus.NOT_ACTIVE]: 2,    
[DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED]: 3,    
[DLXLoanStatus.APPROVED_UPDATED]: 3,    
[DLXLoanStatus.DECLINED]: 3,    
[DLXLoanStatus.EXPIRED]: 3,    
[DLXLoanStatus.FUNDED]: 4,  
};  
};  
const filledDots = filledDotsMap[dlxLoanStatus as DLXLoanStatus] || 1;
  return dlxLoanStatus === DLXLoanStatus.ERROR ? (    
<></>  
) : (    
<SCContainer      
id={`loanprogressstatus-desc-${loanAppId}`}      
aria-labelledby={`loanprogressstatus-desc-${loanAppId}`}      
aria-describedby={t(labelTemplate, {        
apply: applyAlt,        
review: reviewAlt,        
decision: decisionAlt,        
funding: fundingAlt,      
})}>      
<DottedProgressBar        
displayMode={forceDark ? DisplayMode.LIGHT : DisplayMode.DARK}        
filledDots={filledDots}        
totalDots={4}        
legend={[          
t('LOAN_PROGRESS_BAR__LEGEND_APPLY'),          
t('LOAN_PROGRESS_BAR__LEGEND_REVIEW'),          
t('LOAN_PROGRESS_BAR__LEGEND_DECISION'),          
subtype === LoanSubType.CREDIT_CARD ||          
subtype === LoanSubType.PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT ||          
: t('LOAN_PROGRESS_BAR__LEGEND_FUNDING'),        
]}      
/>    
</SCContainer>  
);
};
export { LoanProgressBar };
```

### 291. `C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\atoms\LOXAccordion\LoxAccordion.tsx`
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\atoms\LOXAccordion\LoxAccordion.tsx" type=".tsx" size="6139" lines="173" chars="6139" -->
```typescript
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import vector from '../static/whiteChevron.svg';
import { LOXButton as Button } from '../LOXButton/LOXButton';
import { SCAccordionArrow, SCAccordionContent } from './styles';
import { SCAccordionArrow, SCAccordionContent } from './styles';
import '../loxComponents.css';
interface IAccordion {  
id: string;  
label: string;  
children: React.ReactNode;  
animationTime?: number;  
arrowIcon?: string;  
className?: string;  
badgeText?: string | number;  
clearSelectText?: string;  
maxHeight?: string;  
onClearSelect?: () => void;  
forceOpen?: boolean;  
parentToggle?: boolean;  
onToggle?: (isOpen: boolean) => void;
}
const Messages = {  
close: 'Close section',  
open: 'Open section',
};
/** 
* Renders an accordion component. 
* @param {string} id - ID is required for accessibility purposes 
* Renders an accordion component. 
* @param {string} id - ID is required for accessibility purposes 
* @param {string} label - The label that will render left of the toggle button 
* @param {number} animationTime? - The time (ms) it takes for the section to open and close 
* @param {number} arrowIcon? - The src path for the arrow icon. By default it is a white chevron. 
* @param {string} className? - Optional className for needed css overrides 
* @param {string | number} badgeText? - Optional text that will appear in a badge when the accordion is closed 
* @param {string} clearSelectText? - Optional text for a button in the accordion header when the accordion is open 
* @param {string} maxHeight? - The closing animation will be smoother with a rough estimate of the contents height. By default it is 100vh. 
* @param onClearSelect? - The onClick event for the clearSelectText button 
* @param forceOpen? - Optional prop that forces accordion open (for use with a "open all accordions" function) 
* @param parentToggle? - Optional prop that allows the parent component to control the open/close state of this component 
* @param onToggle? - Optional callback when the accordion is toggled 
*/
export const LoxAccordion = ({  
id,  
label,  
children,  
animationTime = 200,  
arrowIcon = vector,  
badgeText = '',  
className = '',  
clearSelectText = '',  
maxHeight = '120vh',  
onClearSelect,  
forceOpen = false,  
parentToggle,  
onToggle = () => {},
}: IAccordion) => {  
/*        
accordionClass is used to add a class of Opened or Closed when the accordion is done animating        
While it is animating the class will be removed    
*/  
const [accordionClass, setAccordionClass] = useState<'Opened' | 'Closed' | ''>('Opened');  
const [open, setOpen] = useState(true);  
const buttonId = `${id}-button`;  
const contentId = `${id}-content`;  
const clearSelectId = `${id}-clearSelectBtn`;  
const handleChange = useCallback(() => {    
onToggle(open);  
}, [onToggle, open]);
  useEffect(() => {    
const newAccordionClass = open ? 'Opened' : 'Closed';    
if (accordionClass !== newAccordionClass) {      
setAccordionClass('');      
setTimeout(() => {        
setAccordionClass(newAccordionClass);      
}, animationTime);    
setTimeout(() => {        
setAccordionClass(newAccordionClass);      
}, animationTime);    
}  
}, [open]);
  useEffect(() => {    
// only allow parent to force accordion to open, not close    
if (forceOpen) setOpen(forceOpen);    
if (parentToggle !== undefined) {      
setOpen(parentToggle);    
}  
}, [forceOpen, parentToggle]);
  useEffect(() => {    
handleChange();  
}, [open]);  
return (    
<fieldset className='accordionFieldset'>      
<div id={id} className={`Accordion ${accordionClass} ${className}`}>        
{!clearSelectText && (          
<Button            
id={buttonId}            
data-testid={buttonId}            
buttonStyle='Accessibility'            
onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}            
aria-controls={contentId}            
aria-expanded={open}            
aria-label={`Toggle ${label} section`}>            
<legend>              
<h2 className='headerReset hFontReset'>{label}</h2>            
</legend>            
<SCAccordionArrow              
className={`Chevron ${!open ? 'Closed' : ''}`}              
src={arrowIcon}              
alt={open ? Messages.close : Messages.open}              
animationTime={animationTime}            
/>          
</Button>        
)}        
{clearSelectText && (          
<div className='AccordionHeader'>            
<Button              
id={buttonId}              
data-testid={buttonId}              
className='AccordionHeaderLabel'              
buttonStyle='Accessibility'              
onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}              
aria-controls={contentId}              
aria-expanded={open}              
aria-label={`Toggle ${label} section`}>              
<legend>                
<h2 className='headerReset hFontReset'>{label}</h2>              
</legend>            
</Button>            
{open && clearSelectText !== '' && (              
<Button                
id={clearSelectId}                
data-testid={clearSelectId}                
className='AccordionClearSelectBtn'                
buttonStyle='Accessibility'                
aria-label={`${clearSelectText} options in ${label} section`}                
onClick={onClearSelect}>                
{clearSelectText}              
</Button>            
)}            
{!open && badgeText !== '' && <div className='AccordionBadge'>{badgeText}</div>}            
<Button              
id={`${buttonId}-arrow`}              
data-testid={`${buttonId}-arrow`}              
className='AccordionHeaderChevron'              
buttonStyle='Accessibility'              
onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}              
aria-controls={contentId}              
aria-expanded={open}              
aria-label={`Toggle ${label} section`}>              
<SCAccordionArrow                
className={`Chevron ${!open ? 'Closed' : ''}`}                
src={arrowIcon}                
alt={open ? Messages.close : Messages.open}                
animationTime={animationTime}              
/>            
</Button>          
</div>        
)}        
{open && (          
<SCAccordionContent            
id={contentId}            
data-testid={contentId}            
maxHeight={maxHeight}            
animationTime={animationTime}>            
{children}          
</SCAccordionContent>        
)}      
</div>    
</fieldset>  
);
};
</div>    
</fieldset>  
);
};
```

import React, { ReactFragment, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import FocusLock from 'react-focus-lock';
import { LOXButton as Button } from '../LOXButton/LOXButton';
import { Title } from '../Title/title';
import { SubTitle } from '../SubTitle/subTitle';
import closer from '../static/close.svg';
import './loxModal.css';
/** 
* Renders an LOX-based Modal. 
* @param {boolean} show - Set to show or hide the modal 
* @param {void} onClose - function to call in parent when the modal is being closed 
* @param {void} onConfirm - (optional) function to call in parent when the modal's "confirm" button is clicked 
* @param {any} Content - (optional) Children to be placed inside the modal 
* @param {boolean} takeover - (optional) Set to determine if the modal takes over the page entirely 
* @param {string} dimClass - (optional) Class css name for the dimming background 
* @param {any} passThruData - (optional) Data that is to pass through to the Content 
* @param {ReactFragment} Panel - (optional) JSX Fragment for inner content (no pass through data) 
* @param {string} modalTitle - (optional) Title shown in the header in the modal 
* @param {string} modalSubTitle - (optional) Text under the title shown in the header of the modal 
* @param {string} modalSubTitle - (optional) Text under the title shown in the header of the modal 
* @param {string} cancelButtonText - (optional) Text shown in the Cancel button. If this is not defined, the cancel button will not be shown 
* @param {string} confirmButtonText - (optional) Text shown in the Confirm button. If this is not defined, the confirm button will not be shown 
* @param {boolean} flex - (optional) This boolean determines if the modal is build with Flexbox CSS (allowing the middle section to be scrollable) 
*/
type LoxModalProps = {  
show: boolean;  
onClose: (value: any) => void;  
onConfirm?: (value: any) => void | undefined;  
Content?: any;  
takeover: boolean;  
modalClassName?: string;  
dimClass?: string;  
passThruData?: any;  
// setPDF, grouping, and setGrouping are all passthrough values.  
Panel?: ReactFragment;  
modalTitle?: string | ReactFragment;  
modalSubTitle?: string;  
cancelButtonText?: string;  
confirmButtonText?: string;  
flex?: boolean;
};
export const LoxModal = ({  
show,  
show,  
onClose,  
onConfirm,  
Content,  
takeover,  
modalClassName = '',  
dimClass = '',  
passThruData,  
Panel,  
modalTitle = 'Please Confirm',  
modalSubTitle = 'Are you sure you want to proceed with this action?',  
cancelButtonText = 'Cancel',  
confirmButtonText = 'Yes, Confirm',  
flex = false,
}: LoxModalProps): JSX.Element => {  
const mainClassNames = `${takeover ? 'modal-takeover' : 'modal'} ${modalClassName}`;  
const dimClassName = `${dimClass || 'dim'}`;
  const modalMainRef: React.MutableRefObject<HTMLDivElement | null> = useRef(null);  
const closeBtnRef: React.MutableRefObject<HTMLButtonElement | null> = useRef(null);
  useEffect(() => {    
if (modalMainRef.current !== null && closeBtnRef.current !== null) {      
closeBtnRef.current.focus();    
}  
}, [show]);
closeBtnRef.current.focus();    
}  
}, [show]);
  const closeButton = (    
<Button      
ref={closeBtnRef}      
buttonStyle='Accessibility'      
data-testid='closeModal'      
id='modal_close'      
className='closeModal noFilter' // ${!takeover ? 'noFilter' : ''}      
onClick={(e) => {        
onClose(false);        
e.preventDefault();      
}}>      
<img alt='close modal' src={closer} />    
</Button>  
);
  return (    
<FocusLock returnFocus noFocusGuards={false}>      
<div className={`modal-component${flex ? ' flex' : ''}`}>        
{!takeover && show && (          
<div            
className={dimClassName}            
data-testid='dim'            
data-testid='dim'            
role='none'            
onClick={() => onClose(false)}            
onKeyDown={(e) => {              
if (e.code === 'Enter' || e.code === 'Space') return onClose(false);              
return null;            
}}          
/>        
)}        
{show && (          
<div id='popup' /* tabIndex={0} */ ref={modalMainRef} data-testid='modal' className={mainClassNames}>            
<div id={`modal_content${flex ? '_flex' : ''}`} className={`modal-content${flex ? '-flex' : ''}`}>              
{flex ? (                
<div id='modal_content_flex_header' className='modal-content-flex-header'>                  
{closeButton}                
</div>              
) : (                
<>                  
{closeButton}                  
<hr className='separator' />                
</>              
)}              
{Content ? (                
<Content                  
{Content ? (                
<Content                  
onConfirm={onConfirm ? (data: any) => onConfirm(data) : ''}                  
onClose={(data: any) => onClose(data)}                  
passThruData={passThruData}                
/>              
) : (                
<div>                  
<div className='modalTitleContainer'>                    
<Title className='modalTitle'>{modalTitle}</Title>                    
<SubTitle className='modalSubTitle'>{modalSubTitle}</SubTitle>                    
{Panel}                  
</div>
                  <div className='modalButtonContainer'>                    
<Button buttonStyle='Transparent' id='modal_cancel' data-testid='modal_cancel' onClick={onClose}>                      
{cancelButtonText}                    
</Button>
                    <Button                      
className='modalConfirmButton'                      
id='modal_confirm'                      
data-testid='modal_confirm'                      
onClick={onConfirm}>                      
{confirmButtonText}                    
</Button>                  
</div>                
onClick={onConfirm}>                      
{confirmButtonText}                    
</Button>                  
</div>                
</div>              
)}            
</div>          
</div>        
)}      
</div>    
</FocusLock>  
);
};
```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

import { Location } from 'history';
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { LoxModal } from './loxModal';
import { LoxModal } from './loxModal';
const Messages = {  
ModalTitle: 'Uh oh! Your changes have not been saved.',  
ModalSubTitle:    
'If you leave the page without saving your changes, some of your work could be lost. Are you sure you want to leave this page?',  
ModalCancel: 'CANCEL',  
ModalConfirm: 'CONTINUE WITHOUT SAVING',
};
interface Props {  
when?: boolean | undefined; // eslint-disable-line react/no-unused-prop-types  
navigate: (path: string) => void;  
shouldBlockNavigation: (location: Location) => boolean;
}
export const LoxCustomPrompt = ({ navigate, shouldBlockNavigation: _shouldBlockNavigation }: Props) => {  
const [modalVisible, setModalVisible] = useState(false);  
const [lastLocation] = useState<Location | null>(null);  
const [confirmedNavigation, setConfirmedNavigation] = useState(false);  
const closeModal = () => {    
setModalVisible(false);  
};
  const handleConfirmNavigationClick = () => {    
setModalVisible(false);    
setConfirmedNavigation(true);  
};  
  const handleConfirmNavigationClick = () => {    
setModalVisible(false);    
setConfirmedNavigation(true);  
};  
useEffect(() => {    
if (confirmedNavigation && lastLocation) {      
navigate(lastLocation?.pathname);    
}  
}, [confirmedNavigation, lastLocation]);
  return (    
<>      
<LoxModal        
takeover={false}        
onClose={closeModal}        
onConfirm={handleConfirmNavigationClick}        
show={modalVisible}        
modalTitle={Messages.ModalTitle}        
modalSubTitle={Messages.ModalSubTitle}        
cancelButtonText={Messages.ModalCancel}        
confirmButtonText={Messages.ModalConfirm}      
/>    
</>  
);
};

className?: string;  
id?: string | undefined;  
error?: boolean;  
errorMessage?: string;  
maxLength?: number;  
disabled?: boolean;  
defaultValue?: string;  
onChange?: React.ChangeEventHandler<HTMLTextAreaElement>;  
onBlur?: React.FocusEventHandler<HTMLTextAreaElement>;

}
export const LOXTextArea = ({  
// The other TextArea Form element is exported with rename as FormsTextArea, but this is a probable point of failure in the future and has been renamed here

id = undefined,

...restProps
}: ILOXTextArea) => (  
}: ILOXTextArea) => (  
<>    
<textarea      
className={`Textarea ${className} ${error ? 'error' : ''}`}      
rows={rows}      
id={id}      
data-testid={id}      
{...restProps}    
/>    
{error && (      
<div className='inputErrorMessage' id={`${id}-error`} data-testid={`${id}-error`}>        
{errorMessage}      
</div>    
)}  
</>
);

```typescript
import React, { forwardRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles/utility/SCUtilityInput';
import { MASK_FORMAT, applyInputMask } from 'helpers/mask';
import { FieldTypeValues } from 'core/entities/loanApplication';
import { IMaskedInputProps } from './types';
const MaskedInput = forwardRef(  
(    
props: IMaskedInputProps,    
ref: ((instance: HTMLInputElement | null) => void) | React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement> | null | undefined  
props: IMaskedInputProps,    
ref: ((instance: HTMLInputElement | null) => void) | React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement> | null | undefined  
): JSX.Element => {    
const { id, name = '', value = '', placeholder, onChange, disabled, fieldType, className, ...rest } = props;    
const maskedInputChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
const inputVal = event.target.value;      
const caret = applyInputMask(        
inputVal.substring(0, event.target.selectionStart || inputVal.length - 1),        
MASK_FORMAT[fieldType]      
).length;
      const element = event.target;      
window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {        
element.selectionStart = caret;        
element.selectionEnd = caret;      
});
      onChange(name, applyInputMask(inputVal, MASK_FORMAT[fieldType]));    
};
    useEffect(() => {      
if (value && fieldType === FieldTypeValues.PHONE_NUMBER)        
onChange(name, applyInputMask(value, MASK_FORMAT[fieldType]));    
}, []);
    return (      
<SCUtilityInput        
id={id}        
}, []);
    return (      
<SCUtilityInput        
id={id}        
className={className}        
name={name}        
value={applyInputMask(value, MASK_FORMAT[fieldType])}        
placeholder={placeholder}        
onChange={maskedInputChange}        
disabled={disabled}        
ref={ref}        
overrideDisabledColor={fieldType === FieldTypeValues.SSN || fieldType === FieldTypeValues.SSN_WITH_MASK_TOGGLE}        
{...rest}      
/>    
);  
}
);
MaskedInput.displayName = 'MaskedInput';
export { MaskedInput };
```

import React, { ChangeEvent, useContext } from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { ColorModeContext } from 'styles/modes';
import { ErrorMessage } from 'components/atoms/ErrorMessage';
import { IMoneyNumberInputProps } from './types';
import {  
SCCurrencyInput,  
SCMoneyNumberInputContainer,  
SCCurrencyInputButtonContainer,  
SCCurrencyInputButton,
} from './styles';
/** 
* @requires ColorModeContext 
*/
const MoneyNumberInput = ({  
id,  
className,  
labelledBy,  
value,  
interval,  
onChange,  
onBlur,  
hasError,  
readOnly,  
min,
    min.
    max,
        rest
   */  
id: string;  
/**   
* Value of the input.   
*/  
value?: number;  
/**   
* Extra WAI-ARIA text for screen readers (optional).   
*/  
ariaLabel: string;  
/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon input value change.   
*/  
onChange?: (value?: number) => void;  
/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon input blur.   
*/  
onBlur?: (value?: number) => void;  
/**   
* Boolean determining whether to display numbers past the decimal to the user.   
  const increaseValue = (): void => {    
if (value === undefined || interval === undefined) return;    
const increasedNumber = max !== undefined && value + interval >= max ? max : value + interval;    
if ((max && increasedNumber > max) || value === max) return;    
onChangeHandler(name, `${increasedNumber}`);    
onBlur && onBlur(name, `${increasedNumber}`);  
};
  const decreaseValue = (): void => {    
if (value === undefined || interval === undefined) return;    
const minValue = min || 0;    
const decreasedNumber = minValue !== undefined && value - interval <= minValue ? minValue : value - interval;
    if ((minValue && decreasedNumber < minValue) || value === minValue) return;    
onChangeHandler(name, `${decreasedNumber}`);    
onBlur && onBlur(name, `${decreasedNumber}`);  
};
  const onBlurHandler = (e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {    
const { value } = e.target;    
const parsedValue = parseInt(formatValue(value), 10);    
onBlur && !Number.isNaN(parsedValue) && onBlur(name, `${parsedValue}`);  
};
  return (    
<>      
<SCMoneyNumberInputContainer        
className={className}        
mode={mode}        
hasError={typeof hasError === 'string'}        
readOnly={readOnly}>        
<SCCurrencyInput          
id={id}          
value={`${value}`}          
name={name}          
onChange={onChangeHandler}          
onBlur={onBlurHandler}          
{...rest}        
/>        
<SCCurrencyInputButtonContainer>          
<SCCurrencyInputButton            
id={`${id}_button-plus`}            
type='button'            
aria-describedby={labelledBy}            
aria-label={t('STEPPER_INPUT_VIEW__ARIA_INCREASE')}            
onClick={increaseValue}            
increase          
/>          
<SCCurrencyInputButton            
id={`${id}_button-minus`}            
type='button'            
aria-describedby={labelledBy}            
/>          
<SCCurrencyInputButton            
id={`${id}_button-minus`}            
type='button'            
aria-describedby={labelledBy}            
aria-label={t('STEPPER_INPUT_VIEW__ARIA_DECREASE')}            
onClick={decreaseValue}            
increase={false}          
/>        
</SCCurrencyInputButtonContainer>      
</SCMoneyNumberInputContainer>      
{hasError && <ErrorMessage error={hasError} />}    
</>  
);
};
export { MoneyNumberInput };
```

import React, { ChangeEvent, useCallback, useRef, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { ErrorMessage } from 'components/atoms/ErrorMessage';
import { MoneyText } from 'components/atoms/MoneyText';
import { RangeInput } from 'components/atoms/RangeInput';
import { SCErrorSpacer, SCHeroText, SCLabelText, SCFlexRow, SCFlexItem, SCStylizedMoneyInput } from './styles';
import { IMoneySliderProps } from './types';
/** 
* Slider component (`RangeInput`) with linked `MoneyInput`. 
*/
const MoneySlider = (props: IMoneySliderProps): JSX.Element => {  
const {    
value = 0,    
min,    
max,    
step = 1,    
error,    
name,    
min,    
max,    
step = 1,    
error,    
name,    
onChange,    
onBlur,    
hidden,    
ariaLabel = 'Money value in dollars',    
valuesList,    
id,  
} = props;
  /**   
* The current tick mark that indicates where the thumb position should be   
*/  
const [currentTick, setCurrentTick] = useState<number>(0);
  const isMonthlyPayment = name === 'monthlyPayment';
  const getValueInBoundaries = useCallback((value: number): number => Math.max(Math.min(value, max), 0), [max]);
  const findNearest = useCallback(    
(value: number): number => {      
if (!valuesList) return value;      
let diff = max;      
return valuesList.reduce((acc, curr): number => {        
const currDiff = Math.abs(value - curr);        
if (currDiff < diff) {          
diff = currDiff;          
return curr;        
}        
return acc;      
}, 0);    
},    
[valuesList, max]  
);
  const sliderPosition = useRef({    
startPosition: 0,    
endPosition: 0,  
});
  const formatInputValue = (value: string): number => {    
const newString = value.replace(/[$,]/g, '');    
return parseInt(newString, 10);  
};
  const onInputChangeHandler = (name: string, value: string): void => {    
// We need to strip out all non numeric characters    
const formattedValue = formatInputValue(value);    
onChange(name, `${formattedValue}`);  
};
  const onStartChanging = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
  const onInputChangeHandler = (name: string, value: string): void => {    
// We need to strip out all non numeric characters    
const formattedValue = formatInputValue(value);    
onChange(name, `${formattedValue}`);  
};
  const onStartChanging = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
const current = parseInt(e.target.value, 10);      
const sliderValue = isMonthlyPayment && valuesList ? valuesList[current] : current;      
sliderPosition.current.startPosition = isMonthlyPayment && valuesList ? current : sliderValue;
      onChange(name, `${sliderValue}`);    
},    
[onChange, name, valuesList, isMonthlyPayment]  
);
  const onFinishChanging = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
const currentValue = e.target.value;
      if (isMonthlyPayment && valuesList) {        
const parsedValue = valuesList[parseInt(currentValue, 10)];        
onChange(name, `${parsedValue}`);        
onBlur(name, valuesList[parseInt(currentValue, 10)]);      
} else {        
const newFormattedValue = formatInputValue(currentValue);        
const findNearestValue = findNearest(newFormattedValue);        
} else {        
const newFormattedValue = formatInputValue(currentValue);        
const findNearestValue = findNearest(newFormattedValue);        
const valueBounderies = getValueInBoundaries(findNearestValue);
        onChange(name, `${newFormattedValue}`);        
onBlur(name, valueBounderies);      
}    
},    
[onBlur, findNearest, name, onChange, getValueInBoundaries, valuesList, isMonthlyPayment]  
);
  // Get initial thumb position on component mount  
useEffect(() => {    
if (isMonthlyPayment && value && valuesList && valuesList.length > 0) {      
setCurrentTick(valuesList.indexOf(value));    
}  
}, [valuesList, value, name, isMonthlyPayment]);
  return !hidden ? (    
<>      
{error ? <ErrorMessage error={error} showIcon /> : <SCErrorSpacer />}      
<SCHeroText>        
<SCStylizedMoneyInput          
id={`${id}__text-input`}          
className={error ? 'invalid' : ''}          
name={name}          
aria-label={ariaLabel}          
value={value ? `${value}` : ''}          
onChange={onInputChangeHandler}          
onBlur={onFinishChanging}          
fractionDigits={isMonthlyPayment ? 2 : undefined}          
disabled={!!valuesList}          
data-testid={`${id}__text-input`}        
/>      
</SCHeroText>      
<SCFlexRow>        
<SCFlexItem grow>          
<RangeInput            
id={`${id}__range-input`}            
ariaLabel={ariaLabel}            
min={min}            
max={max}            
value={!isMonthlyPayment ? value : currentTick}            
step={step}            
name={name}            
valuesList={valuesList}            
onChange={onStartChanging}            
onKeyDown={onStartChanging}            
onKeyUp={onFinishChanging}            
onMouseDown={onStartChanging}            
onMouseUp={onFinishChanging}            
onTouchStart={onStartChanging}            
onTouchEnd={onFinishChanging}          
/>        
</SCFlexItem>        
{!isMonthlyPayment && (          
<SCFlexItem>            
<SCLabelText>              
<MoneyText dollarValue={max} shortenThousands />            
</SCLabelText>          
</SCFlexItem>        
)}      
</SCFlexRow>    
</>  
) : (    
<></>  
);
};
export { MoneySlider };
```

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { useCombobox, useMultipleSelection } from 'downshift';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import { IOption } from 'core/entities/loanApplication';
import { escapeNewRegExpString } from 'helpers/utils';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles/utility/SCUtilityInput';
import { shouldFilterOnKeyUp, NonFilterKeys } from 'types/generics';
import {  
SCSelectedItemsGroup,  
SCMSCombobox,  
SCDismissIcon,  
SCInputWrap,  
SCMenu,  
SCMenuItem,  
SCMenuToggle,  
SCNoOptionsMessage,  
SCTagButton,  
SCTagContent,
} from './styles';
import { IMultiSelectComboboxProps } from './types';
import { useMultiSelectCombobox } from './useMultiSelectCombobox';
/** 
* This element creates a dropdown menu and allows a user to create and edit a list of selections from said dropdown menu. 
*/
const MultiSelectCombobox = ({  
id,  
name,  
options,  
name,  
options,  
initialSelectedItems,  
placeholder,  
disabled,  
iconType = ICON_TYPES.ARROW_DOWN_FIT,  
isReadOnly,  
shouldTruncateTag = false,  
onChange,  
onBlur,  
...rest
}: IMultiSelectComboboxProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTranslation();  
const comboboxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);  
const menuRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement | null>(null);
  const { selectedItems, getDropdownProps, setSelectedItems, getSelectedItemProps, removeSelectedItem } =    
useMultipleSelection({      
initialSelectedItems,    
});  
const { itemsFilter, setItemsFilter, stateReducer, handleChange, handleBlur, handleHighlightChange } =    
useMultiSelectCombobox(options, selectedItems, setSelectedItems, onChange, onBlur);
  const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
if (!a.order || !b.order) {      
return 0;    
  const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
if (!a.order || !b.order) {      
return 0;    
}    
return a.order - b.order;  
});  
const inputItems: IOption[] = selectedItems    
? sortedOptions.filter((option) =>        
new RegExp(`\\b${escapeNewRegExpString(itemsFilter)}`, 'i').test(option.label ?? '')      
)    
: sortedOptions;
  const {    
getInputProps,    
getItemProps,    
getMenuProps,    
getToggleButtonProps,    
highlightedIndex,    
isOpen,    
openMenu,    
selectedItem,    
setHighlightedIndex,  
} = useCombobox({    
id,    
selectedItem: null,    
id,    
selectedItem: null,    
items: inputItems,    
stateReducer,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
isItemDisabled: (item) => !!item.disabled,  
});
  useEffect(() => {    
handleChange();  
}, [selectedItems]);
  return (    
<>      
<SCMSCombobox        
ref={comboboxRef}        
className='MsCombobox'        
onBlur={handleBlur}        
tabIndex={-1} // Traps the focus inside the Combobox, when switching from input to button        
onClick={() => {          
/**           
* Brief timeout used to give SCMenu ref an opportunity to open           
* and have its bottom position be correctly evaluated.           
*/          
setTimeout(() => {            
const menuBottom =              
menuRef.current && menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()                
? menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect().bottom                
: 0;            
// Scroll into view only if necessary to show options out of view.            
if (menuBottom >= window.innerHeight && comboboxRef.current) {              
comboboxRef.current?.scrollIntoView();            
}          
}, 10);        
}}>        
<SCInputWrap isOpen={isOpen} className='MsCombobox__input-wrap'>          
<SCUtilityInput            
{...getInputProps(              
getDropdownProps({                
id,                
name,                
onFocus: (e: React.FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {                  
if (!e.target.value && !isOpen) {                    
openMenu();                  
} else {                    
e.target.select();                  
}                
},                
onKeyDown: (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {                  
if (e.key === NonFilterKeys.UP_ARROW_KEY || e.key === NonFilterKeys.DOWN_ARROW_KEY) {                    
handleHighlightChange(e, id);                  
}                
},                
onKeyUp: (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {                  
const inputElement = e.target as HTMLInputElement; // Must cast due to incorrect type coming from the lib.                  
if (shouldFilterOnKeyUp(e.key) && inputElement.value !== selectedItem?.label) {                    
setItemsFilter(inputElement.value ?? '');                  
}                
},                
disabled,                
...rest,                
placeholder,              
})            
)}            
readOnly={isReadOnly}            
className='MsCombobox__input'          
/>          
<SCMenuToggle            
isOpen={isOpen}            
{...getDropdownProps(              
getToggleButtonProps({                
className: 'MsCombobox__menu-toggle',                
tabIndex: 0,                
id: `${id}-toggle`,                
onClick: (): void => {                  
requestAnimationFrame(() => {                    
setHighlightedIndex(-1);                  
});                
},                
disabled,              
})            
)}            
aria-label={t('MS_COMBOBOX__MENU_TOGGLE_ARIA')}            
data-testid='menu-toggle-button'>            
<Icon name={iconType} size={14} />          
</SCMenuToggle>        
</SCInputWrap>        
<SCMenu          
isOpen={isOpen}          
{...getMenuProps({            
className: 'MsCombobox__menu',            
ref: menuRef,          
})}>          
{isOpen && (            
<>              
{inputItems.length > 0 ? (                
inputItems.map((item, index) => {                  
return (                    
<SCMenuItem                      
key={item.value}                      
isHighlighted={highlightedIndex === index}                      
{...getItemProps({                        
item,                        
index,                        
className: 'MsCombobox__menu-item',                      
})}>                      
{item.label}                      
{selectedItems.some((selectedItem: IOption) => selectedItem.value === item.value) && (                        
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} data-testid='selected-checkmark' />                      
)}                    
</SCMenuItem>                  
);                
})              
) : (                
<SCNoOptionsMessage>{t('MS_COMBOBOX__NO_RESULTS').toString()}</SCNoOptionsMessage>              
)}            
</>          
)}        
</SCMenu>      
</SCMSCombobox>      
<SCSelectedItemsGroup>        
{selectedItems.map((selectedItem: IOption, index: number) => (          
<SCTagButton            
isSecondary            
onClick={() => {              
removeSelectedItem(selectedItem as never);            
}}            
key={`selected-item-${selectedItem.label}`}            
id={`selected-item-${selectedItem.label}`}>            
<SCTagContent              
shouldTruncateTag={shouldTruncateTag}              
{...getSelectedItemProps({ selectedItem: selectedItem as never, index })}>              
{selectedItem?.label}            
</SCTagContent>            
<SCDismissIcon>&#10005;</SCDismissIcon>          
</SCTagButton>        
))}      
</SCSelectedItemsGroup>    
</>  
);
};
MultiSelectCombobox.displayName = 'MultiSelectCombobox';
export { MultiSelectCombobox };

export interface IMultiSelectComboboxProps {  
id: string;

disabled?: boolean;

iconType?: ICON_TYPES;  
/** Is not implemented */  
isReadOnly?: boolean;

shouldTruncateTag?: boolean;  
/** The full list of options that can be selected from the dropdown */  
options: IOption[];  
/** Can be used to pass items which are selected by default. Should be passed an empty array if no default selections are desired */  
initialSelectedItems: IOption[];  
onChange: (selectedItems: IOption[]) => void;  
onBlur?: () => void;
}

import { IOption } from 'core/entities/loanApplication';
import { useCombobox, UseComboboxState } from 'downshift';
import { escapeNewRegExpString } from 'helpers';
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
export const useMultiSelectCombobox = (  
options: IOption[],  
selectedItems: IOption[],  
setSelectedItems: (items: never[]) => void,  
onChange: (selectedItems: IOption[]) => void,  
onBlur: (() => void) | undefined
) => {  
const [itemsFilter, setItemsFilter] = useState('');  
const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
const [itemsFilter, setItemsFilter] = useState('');  
const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
if (!a.order || !b.order) {      
return 0;    
}    
return a.order - b.order;  
});
  const stateReducer = (_state: UseComboboxState<IOption>, actionAndChanges: any): any => {    
const { type, changes } = actionAndChanges;    
const { selectedItem }: { selectedItem: IOption; inputValue: string } = changes;    
const isValidSelection = selectedItems.some((item: IOption) => item.value === selectedItem?.value);
    switch (type) {      
case useCombobox.stateChangeTypes.InputKeyDownEnter:      
case useCombobox.stateChangeTypes.ItemClick:        
if (!isValidSelection) {          
setSelectedItems([...(selectedItems as never[]), selectedItem as never]);        
} else {          
setSelectedItems(selectedItems.filter((item: IOption) => item?.value !== selectedItem?.value) as never[]);        
}        
return changes;      
default:        
return changes;    
}  
};
return changes;    
}  
};
  const inputItems: IOption[] = selectedItems    
? sortedOptions.filter((option) =>        
new RegExp(`\\b${escapeNewRegExpString(itemsFilter)}`, 'i').test(option.label ?? '')      
)    
: sortedOptions;
  const handleChange = useCallback(() => {    
onChange(selectedItems);    
setItemsFilter('');  
}, [onChange, selectedItems]);
  const handleBlur = (e: React.FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLButtonElement | HTMLDivElement>): void => {    
// Using a timeout, because the blur event will fire way faster than onInputValueChange    
// and we will get a validation error for a split second, for a valid value.    
// Also, tabing from the input to the button will fire the validation.    
// Firing onBlur only if the next active element, doesn't contain a class that starts with Combobox    
// opened an issue in `downshift` and will follow it for a better approach:    
// https://github.com/downshift-js/downshift/issues/1095#issuecomment-672323045    
const { tagName } = e.target;    
if (tagName === 'BUTTON' || tagName === 'INPUT') {      
requestAnimationFrame(() => {        
if (!document.activeElement?.className.includes('Combobox')) {          
const selectedItem = inputItems.find((item) => item.label?.toLowerCase() === itemsFilter.toLocaleLowerCase());
}
          onBlur?.();        
}      
});    
}  
};

};

import React from 'react';
import { SCNavBarLink, SCNavLink } from './styles';
import { INavBarLinkProps } from './types';
/** 
* A styled version of react-router's NavLink, for use in the main navigation bar. 
*/
export const NavBarLink = ({ route, text, displayMode }: INavBarLinkProps): JSX.Element => {  
return (    
<SCNavBarLink $displayMode={displayMode}>      
<SCNavLink to={route} $displayMode={displayMode}>        
{text}      
</SCNavLink>    
</SCNavBarLink>  
);
};

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Direction } from 'core/enums';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { SCNavHorizontalButton } from './styles';
import { INavHorizontalButtonProps } from './types';
import { ReactComponent as RightArrowDark } from './svg/arrow-right-grayscale-04.svg';
import { ReactComponent as LeftArrowDark } from './svg/arrow-left-grayscale-04.svg';
import { ReactComponent as RightArrowLight } from './svg/arrow-right-contrastblack.svg';
import { ReactComponent as LeftArrowLight } from './svg/arrow-left-contrastblack.svg';
import { ReactComponent as RightArrowDisabled } from './svg/arrow-right-grayscale-06.svg';
import { ReactComponent as LeftArrowDisabled } from './svg/arrow-left-grayscale-06.svg';
const arrowSVGs = {  
[DisplayMode.DARK]: {    
disabled: {      
disabled: {      
[Direction.RIGHT]: RightArrowDisabled,      
[Direction.LEFT]: LeftArrowDisabled,    
},    
default: {      
[Direction.RIGHT]: RightArrowDark,      
[Direction.LEFT]: LeftArrowDark,    
},  
},  
[DisplayMode.LIGHT]: {    
disabled: {      
[Direction.RIGHT]: RightArrowDisabled,      
[Direction.LEFT]: LeftArrowDisabled,    
},    
default: {      
[Direction.RIGHT]: RightArrowLight,      
[Direction.LEFT]: LeftArrowLight,    
},  
},
};
/** 
* NavHorizontalButton 
* @summary 
* @param id 
* @param id 
* @param ariaLabel 
* @param displayMode 
* @param direction 
* @param disabled 
* Horizontal arrow navigation button 
* displayMode (light or dark) and direction (left or right) props linked to DISPLAYMODE and DIRECTION enums 
* onClick function passed in as prop 
* id and ariaLabel are required props 
*/
const NavHorizontalButton = ({  
id,  
ariaLabel,  
displayMode = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
disabled = false,  
direction = Direction.LEFT,  
onClick = (): void => {},
}: INavHorizontalButtonProps): JSX.Element => {  
const arrowType = disabled ? 'disabled' : 'default';  
const ArrowComponent = arrowSVGs[displayMode][arrowType][direction];  
return (    
<SCNavHorizontalButton      
id={id}      
aria-label={ariaLabel}      
direction={direction}      
displayMode={displayMode}      
disabled={disabled}      
onClick={onClick}>      
<ArrowComponent />    
</SCNavHorizontalButton>  
);
};
export { NavHorizontalButton };
```

* Displays the product image for an offer. 
*/
const OfferImage = ({ id, className, size, mediaUrl, mediaBgColor }: IOfferImageProps): JSX.Element | null => {  
// Note that an empty `alt` attribute is preferred for decorative images, which is the case here.  
const imageElement = (    
<SCImg id={id} alt='' className={className} src={mediaUrl} size={size} hasMediaBgColor={!!mediaBgColor} />  
);  
if (mediaUrl && mediaBgColor) {    
return (      
<SCImgContainer className={className} size={size} bgColor={mediaBgColor}>        
{imageElement}      
</SCImgContainer>    
);  
}  
return mediaUrl ? imageElement : null;
};
`;

import React from 'react';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles/utility/SCUtilityInput';
import { IPercentageInputProps } from './types';
import { SCMaskedValue, SCPercentageInput } from './styles';
import { formatPercentage } from '../../../helpers/formatters';
/** 
* vary large percentages seem to interfere with html5 validation 
*/
*/
const DEFAULT_MAX_VALUE = 10000;
/** 
* 
* PercentageInput 
* @summary 
* Percentage input field that takes in name, value, fractionDigits, and onChange function as props 
* onChange is called with (name, value) and will return a string of format 'XX.YY%' 
* Inputs are automatically changed to be % format 
*/
const PercentageInput = ({  
name = '',  
value = '',  
onChange,  
error,  
fractionDigits = 0,  
maxValue = DEFAULT_MAX_VALUE,  
minValue = undefined,  
...rest
}: IPercentageInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const handleChange = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {    
const inputVal = event.target.value;    
if (inputVal && (event?.target?.validationMessage || !event?.target?.validity?.valid)) {      
return;    
const inputVal = event.target.value;    
if (inputVal && (event?.target?.validationMessage || !event?.target?.validity?.valid)) {      
return;    
}    
/**     
* html5 validation does not prevent the scenario where     
* fraction digits > 0     
* value = x.00000000     
*/    
const inputValFractionDigits = (inputVal ?? '').split('.')[1]?.length;
    if (inputValFractionDigits && inputValFractionDigits > fractionDigits) {      
return;    
}    
onChange(name, inputVal);  
};
  const inputClassName = `${rest.className ?? ''}${error ? ' invalid' : ''}`;  
const cleanedValue = value.replace('%', '');
  return (    
<SCPercentageInput>      
<SCUtilityInput        
{...rest}        
name={name}        
autoComplete='off'        
value={cleanedValue}        
onChange={handleChange}        
inputMode={fractionDigits === 0 ? 'numeric' : 'decimal'}        
type='number'        
step={1 / 10 ** fractionDigits}        
max={maxValue}        
min={minValue}        
className={inputClassName}      
/>      
{cleanedValue && (        
<SCMaskedValue>          
{cleanedValue}          
<span>%</span>        
</SCMaskedValue>      
)}    
</SCPercentageInput>  
);
};
PercentageInput.displayName = 'PercentageInput';
export { PercentageInput, formatPercentage };
```

```typescript
import { Size } from 'core/enums';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import React from 'react';
import { SCRadioInputWrapper, ScRadioInput, SCLabel } from './styles';
import { IRadioInputProps } from './types';
/** 
* Basic radio input component. 
* Consists of a visible radio and uses a hidden input for accessibility. 
*/
const RadioInput = ({  
ariaLabel,  
id,  
name,  
children,  
value,  
checked = false,  
disabled = false,  
colorScheme = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
size = Size.DEFAULT,  
colorScheme = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
size = Size.DEFAULT,  
className = '',  
onChange = (): void => {},  
changeOnChecked = false,
}: IRadioInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
// Returning a the field value instead of the input field event, because it was build this way initially  
// and returning the input field event will break some existing functionality  
const handleChange = (): void => {    
onChange(value);  
};
  // This handles if the radio button is already checked to still fire the onChange  
const handleChecked = (): void => {    
if (checked && value) {      
onChange(value);    
}  
};
  return (    
<SCRadioInputWrapper className={`${className} ${disabled ? 'disabled' : ''}`} size={size} colorScheme={colorScheme}>      
<ScRadioInput        
id={id}        
name={name}        
checked={checked}        
onChange={handleChange}        
onClick={changeOnChecked ? handleChecked : undefined}        
disabled={disabled}        
aria-checked={checked}        
aria-label={ariaLabel}      
/>      
<SCLabel        
disabled={disabled}        
htmlFor={id}        
size={size}        
checked={checked}        
colorScheme={colorScheme}        
id={`${id}Label`}>        
{children}      
</SCLabel>    
</SCRadioInputWrapper>  
);
};
export { RadioInput };
```

display: flex;  
align-items: top;
  ${({ size }): FlattenSimpleInterpolation =>    
(size === Size.LARGE &&      
css`

(size === Size.SMALL &&      
css`

line-height: 1.43;      
`) ||    
`) ||    
css`      
align-items: center;      
font-size: 14px;      
padding: 12px 16px 12px 16px;      
line-height: 1.43;    
`};
  color: ${({ colorScheme, checked, disabled }): IPropsHook =>    
(colorScheme === DisplayMode.DARK &&

(disabled && (checked ? Theme.getColor('contrastGreyscale08') : Theme.getColor('contrastGreyscale06'))) ||    
Theme.getColor('contrastBlack')};
  /* Styling ::before as the Radio button */

    input:focus + & {

import { IRangeInputProps } from './types';
import { SCRangeInput, SCRangeInputContainer, SCRangeInputMarker, SCRangeInputMarkerContainer } from './styles';
const isInRange = (value: number, min: number, max: number): boolean => value >= min && value <= max;
/** 
* Basic slider (range input) component. 
*/
const RangeInput = (props: IRangeInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const {    
min,    
max,    
step,    
value,    
onChange,    
ariaLabel,    
onKeyDown,    
onKeyUp,    
name,    
onMouseDown,    
onMouseUp,    
onTouchStart,    
onTouchEnd,    
valuesList,    
id,  
} = props;
onTouchEnd,    
valuesList,    
id,  
} = props;
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const isMonthlyPayment = name === 'monthlyPayment';
  const position = useRef({    
start: 0,  
});
  const debouncedDispatch = useRef(    
debounce((id: string, value: number) => {      
document.dispatchEvent(        
new CustomEvent('rangeInputChange', {          
detail: {            
id,            
direction: position.current.start > value ? 'down' : 'up',          
},        
})      
);    
}, 200)  
).current;
  const updateSliderInternalValue = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
const numberValue = parseInt((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value, 10);      
if (value && position.current.start !== value) {        
debouncedDispatch(id, value);      
}
      if (name === 'monthlyPayment' && valuesList) {        
isInRange(valuesList[numberValue], valuesList[0], valuesList[valuesList.length - 1]) && onChange(e);      
} else {        
isInRange(numberValue, min, max) && onChange(e);      
}    
},    
[onChange, min, max, position, value, id, debouncedDispatch, name, valuesList]  
);
  const logMouseDown = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
position.current.start = value || 0;      
if (onMouseDown) onMouseDown(e);    
},    
[onMouseDown, position, value]  
);
  const logTouchStart = useCallback(    
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
position.current.start = value || 0;      
onTouchStart(e);    
},    
[onTouchStart, position, value]  
(e: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
position.current.start = value || 0;      
onTouchStart(e);    
},    
[onTouchStart, position, value]  
);
  return (    
<SCRangeInputContainer>      
<SCRangeInput        
id={id}        
min={min}        
max={max}        
step={step}        
name={name}        
ref={inputRef}        
value={value || 0}        
onKeyDown={onKeyDown}        
onKeyUp={onKeyUp}        
onMouseDown={logMouseDown}        
onMouseUp={onMouseUp}        
onTouchStart={logTouchStart}        
onTouchEnd={onTouchEnd}        
onChange={updateSliderInternalValue}        
ariaLabel={ariaLabel}      
/>      
{isMonthlyPayment && valuesList && valuesList.length > 0 && (        
<SCRangeInputMarkerContainer>          
{valuesList.map((value: number) => (            
<SCRangeInputMarker key={value} id={`range-input-marker__${value}`} />          
))}        
</SCRangeInputMarkerContainer>      
)}    
</SCRangeInputContainer>  
);
};
export { RangeInput };
```

import React from 'react';
import styled, { css } from 'styled-components';
import { Theme } from 'styles';
import { IInputProps } from './types';
// TODO get from theme property, wrap with default theme
const contrastGreyscale04 = '#c8ced1';
const contrastBlack = '#262c30';
// spacing and size
const trackHeight = 4;
const thumbBorderRadius = '20px';
const totalHeight = 20;
const thumbHeight = 24;
const thumbWidth = 40;
// color
const thumbColor = contrastBlack;
const trackFillColor = Theme.assignGroup('G1', '#262c30');
const trackColor = contrastGreyscale04;
const rangeTrackStyle = css`  
position: relative;  
box-sizing: border-box;  
width: 100%;  
height: ${trackHeight}px;  
background: ${trackColor};
height: ${trackHeight}px;  
background: ${trackColor};
`;
const rangeThumbStyle = css`  
z-index: 1;  
box-sizing: border-box;  
position: relative;  
border-radius: ${thumbBorderRadius};  
border: none;  
height: ${thumbHeight}px;  
width: ${thumbWidth}px;  
overflow: visible;  
background: ${thumbColor};  
-webkit-appearance: none;  
cursor: pointer;
`;
const firefoxMicrosoftThumbStyle = css`  
${rangeThumbStyle} margin-top: 0px;
`;
export const focusStyle = css`  
outline: none;  
::-moz-focus-inner {    
border: 0;  
}  
outline: none;  
::-moz-focus-inner {    
border: 0;  
}  
&::-webkit-slider-thumb {    
box-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px ${thumbColor};  
}
`;
export const SCRangeInputContainer = styled.div`  
position: relative;  
width: 100%;
`;
export const SCRangeInputMarkerContainer = styled.div`  
position: absolute;  
top: 6px;  
left: 0;  
display: flex;  
justify-content: space-between;  
width: 100%;  
padding: 0 ${thumbWidth / 2}px;  
pointer-events: none;
`;
/** 
* Since we can't use a datalist element because of lack of flexibility 
* Since we can't use a datalist element because of lack of flexibility 
* in styling, we need to create a custom tick and calculate its 
* position based on the width of the input itself. 
*/
export const SCRangeInputMarker = styled.span`  
height: 8px;  
width: 8px;  
border-radius: 50%;  
pointer-events: none;  
background: ${Theme.getColor('contrastGreyscale08')};
`;
/** 
* We have to forward the ref so we can grab the width of the input itself, 
* otherwise we have no way of positioning the tick marks properly. 
*/
const RangeInput = React.forwardRef<HTMLInputElement, IInputProps>(({ ariaLabel, ...rest }: IInputProps, ref) => (  
<input aria-label={ariaLabel} type='range' ref={ref} {...rest} />
));
RangeInput.displayName = 'RangeInput';
export const SCRangeInput = styled(RangeInput).attrs(({ value = 0, min = 0, max = 100 }) => {  
const percent = ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100;  
return {    
style: {      
const percent = ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100;  
return {    
style: {      
// Dynamic filled track for Chrome/Safari. We use the attrs method for the dynamic background so only the inline style changes and not the CSS class      
'--track-fill-background': `linear-gradient(to right, var(--track-fill-color) 0%, var(--track-fill-color) ${percent}%, ${trackColor} ${percent}%, ${trackColor} 100%)`,    
},  
};
})`  
box-sizing: border-box;  
position: relative;  
-webkit-appearance: none;  
border-color: transparent;  
height: ${totalHeight}px;  
width: 100%;  
padding: 0px;  
cursor: pointer;  
--track-fill-color: ${trackFillColor};  
&:focus {    
outline: none;  
}  
:invalid {    
box-shadow: none;  
}  
&::-moz-focus-inner {    
border: none;  
}  
&::-moz-focus-outer {    
border: none;  
}  
&::-webkit-slider-runnable-track {    
${rangeTrackStyle}    
background: var(--track-fill-background);  
}  
&::-webkit-slider-thumb {    
margin-top: -${(thumbHeight - trackHeight) * 0.5}px;    
${rangeThumbStyle}    
${css`      
&:hover {        
box-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px ${thumbColor};      
}    
`}    
${Theme.getMediaQuery(Theme.MD, Theme.SM)} {      
margin-top: -8px;      
height: ${totalHeight}px;    
}  
}  
&::-moz-range-track {    
${rangeTrackStyle}  
}  
::-moz-range-progress {    
background: var(--track-fill-color);    
border-color: transparent;  
}  
&::-moz-range-thumb {    
${firefoxMicrosoftThumbStyle}  
}  
&::-ms-thumb {    
${firefoxMicrosoftThumbStyle}    
border-radius: 50%;  
}  
${css`    
&:hover::-moz-range-thumb {      
box-shadow: 0px 0px 0px 2px ${thumbColor};    
}  
`}  
&::-ms-track {    
${rangeTrackStyle}    
border-color: transparent;    
color: transparent;    
border-right: 1px solid ${trackColor};    
margin-right: 1px;  
}  
&::-ms-fill-lower {    
background: var(--track-fill-color);    
border-color: transparent;  
}  
&::-ms-fill-upper {    
background: ${trackColor};    
border-color: transparent;  
}
  &:focus {    
${focusStyle}  
}
`;
```

import React from 'react';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { translation } from 'i18n';
import {  
SCReportModuleError,  
SCReportModuleErrorTitle,  
SCReportModuleContentWrapper,  
SCReportModule,  
SCReportModuleTopLine,  
SCReportModuleTitle,  
SCReportModulePretitle,  
SCReportModuleToggleContainer,  
SCReportModuleDownloadContainer,  
SCReportModuleDownloadButton,  
SCReportModuleChildrenContainer,  
SCReportModuleChildrenWrapper,  
SCReportModuleToggle,
} from './styles';
import { IReportModuleProps, ONE_COLUMN, TWO_COLUMN } from './types';
import downloadIcon from '../Icon/svg/download.svg';
// Based on LOX Report Module by Brandon Klezynski
const ReportModuleErrorMessage = (isEmpty: boolean, isDateRangeUsed: boolean) => {  
const { t } = useTranslation();  
let message = '';  
if (isEmpty) {    
message = isDateRangeUsed      
? t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_NO_DATA_DATE_RANGE)      
message = isDateRangeUsed      
? t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_NO_DATA_DATE_RANGE)      
: t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_NO_DATA_NO_DATE_RANGE);  
} else {    
message = t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_DATA_NOT_AVAILABLE);  
}  
return (    
<SCReportModuleError>      
<SCReportModuleErrorTitle>{message}</SCReportModuleErrorTitle>    
</SCReportModuleError>  
);
};
const widthConvert = (width: number | '1-column' | '2-column' | undefined) => {  
switch (width) {    
case '1-column':      
return ONE_COLUMN;    
case '2-column':    
case undefined:      
return TWO_COLUMN;    
default:      
return width;  
}
};
export const ReportModule = ({  
export const ReportModule = ({  
children,  
title,  
pretitle,  
className,  
id,  
isDateRangeUsed,  
loaded,  
width,  
height,  
padding,  
toggleData,  
marginBottom,  
borderRadius,  
chartTopMargin,  
onDownloadClick,  
onToggleSelect,
}: IReportModuleProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTranslation();  
const moduleWidth: number = widthConvert(width);
  return (    
<SCReportModule      
width={moduleWidth}      
height={height}      
height={height}      
borderRadius={borderRadius}      
marginBottom={marginBottom}      
id={id}      
data-testid={id}      
className={className}>      
{loaded < 0 && ReportModuleErrorMessage(loaded === -2, isDateRangeUsed === true)}      
<SCReportModuleContentWrapper id={`ReportModule${id}`} padding={padding}>        
<SCReportModuleTopLine>          
{(title || pretitle) && (            
<SCReportModuleTitle id={`chartTitle_${id}`}>              
<SCReportModulePretitle id={`countTotal_${id}`}>{pretitle}</SCReportModulePretitle>              
{title}            
</SCReportModuleTitle>          
)}          
{toggleData && (            
<SCReportModuleToggleContainer>              
<SCReportModuleToggle                
id={`${id}ToggleButton`}                
data={toggleData}                
defaultChecked={toggleData[0].value}                
label={`${t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_TOGGLE_LABEL)} ${title}`}                
onSelect={onToggleSelect}              
/>            
</SCReportModuleToggleContainer>          
)}          
{onDownloadClick && (            
<SCReportModuleDownloadContainer withToggle={!!toggleData}>              
<SCReportModuleDownloadButton id={`downloadButton_${id}`} isSecondary onClick={onDownloadClick}>                
<img src={downloadIcon} alt={`${t(translation.REPORTS_MODULE_DOWNLOAD_ALT)} ${title}`} />              
</SCReportModuleDownloadButton>            
</SCReportModuleDownloadContainer>          
)}        
</SCReportModuleTopLine>        
<SCReportModuleChildrenContainer chartTopMargin={chartTopMargin}>          
<SCReportModuleChildrenWrapper>{loaded !== 0 && children}</SCReportModuleChildrenWrapper>        
</SCReportModuleChildrenContainer>      
</SCReportModuleContentWrapper>    
</SCReportModule>  
);
};
```

export const SidebarNavLink = ({ route, text, id, isDisabled = false }: ISidebarNavLinkProps): JSX.Element => {  
return (

{isDisabled ? (        
<SCDisabledLink>{text}</SCDisabledLink>      
) : (        
<SCSidebarNavLink to={route} id={id}>          
{text}        
</SCSidebarNavLink>      
)}    
</SCSidebarNavListItem>  
);
};

```typescript
/** 
* Properties interface for the `SidebarNavLink` component. 
*/
export interface ISidebarNavLinkProps {  
/**   
* The route to use for the underlying `NavLink`.   
*/  
route: string;  
/**   
* The text to display.   
*/  
text: string;  
/**   
* Optional id used for qa testing.   
text,  
ariaLabel,  
expanded = false,  
onChangeActive,  
onChangeExpand,  
subLinks,
}: ISidebarNavSubLinksProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { pathname } = useLocation();  
const active = subLinks.some(({ route }) => matchPath(pathname, route));  
const toggleButtonId = `${id}-toggle`;  
const subLinksId = `${id}-sub-links`;
  useEffect(() => {    
onChangeActive?.({ id, active, expanded, subLinks });  
}, [active]);
  const expandedIcon: ICON_TYPES = active ? ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_UP : ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_UP_GRAY;  
const collapsedIcon: ICON_TYPES = active ? ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON : ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_DOWN_GRAY;  
return (    
<SCLi id={id} active={active} expanded={expanded}>      
<SCExpandToggle        
id={toggleButtonId}        
aria-controls={subLinksId}        
aria-expanded={expanded}        
aria-label={ariaLabel}        
onClick={() => onChangeExpand({ id, active, expanded: !expanded, subLinks })}>        
<span>{text}</span>        
<SCArrowWrap>          
<Icon name={expanded ? expandedIcon : collapsedIcon} />        
</SCArrowWrap>      
</SCExpandToggle>      
<SCExpandWrap id={subLinksId} expanded={expanded} itemCount={subLinks.length}>        
{subLinks.map(({ key, route, text, isDisabled }) => (          
<SCBulletPoint key={key}>            
<SCNavLink to={route} disabled={isDisabled}>              
{text}            
</SCNavLink>          
</SCBulletPoint>        
))}      
</SCExpandWrap>    
</SCLi>  
);
};
export { SidebarNavSubLinks };
```

import type { ISidebarNavLinkProps } from '../SidebarNavLink/types';
export interface ISidebarNavSubLink extends ISidebarNavLinkProps {  
/**   
* The React key attribute   
*/  
key: string;
}
export interface IOnChangeProps {  
/**   
* id property for this component.   
*/  
id: string;  
/**   
* Indicates if one of the child navigation links is active.   
*/  
active: boolean;  
/**   
* expanded property for this component, can be either current value or next value.   
*/  
expanded: boolean;  
/**   
* subLinks property for this component.   
*/  
*/  
subLinks: ISidebarNavSubLink[];
}
/** 
* Properties interface for the `SidebarNavSubLinks` component. 
*/
export interface ISidebarNavSubLinksProps {  
/**   
* id attribute to apply to the element, and use as a prefix for some child elements.   
*/  
id: string;  
/**   
* Text of the main navigation element that expands/collapses the sub nav.   
*/  
text: string;  
/**   
* Optional ARIA label to describe the main navigation element.   
*/  
ariaLabel?: string;  
/**   
* Indicates if the sub navigation links are expanded or collapsed.   
*/  
expanded?: boolean;  
/**   
/**   
* Fires when the router's location changes and the active state changes.   
* @param props - the new value of active, plus the current state for the rest   
*/  
onChangeActive?: (props: IOnChangeProps) => void;  
/**   
* Fires when user clicks the section to expand or collapse it.   
* @param props - the new value of expanded, plus the current state for the rest   
*/  
onChangeExpand: (props: IOnChangeProps) => void;  
/**   
* Defines the links to show in the sub navigation section.   
*/  
subLinks: ISidebarNavSubLink[];
}
export interface IUseSingleNavSubLinksReturn {  
/**   
* Indicates if the sub navigation links are expanded or collapsed.   
*/  
expanded: boolean;  
/**   
* Fires when the router's location changes and the active state changes.   
* @param props - the new value of active, plus the current state for the rest   
*/  
onChangeActive: (props: IOnChangeProps) => void;  
*/  
subLinks: ISidebarNavSubLink[];
}
/** 
* Properties interface for the `SidebarNavSubLinks` component. 
*/
export interface ISidebarNavSubLinksProps {  
/**   
* id attribute to apply to the element, and use as a prefix for some child elements.   
*/  
id: string;  
/**   
* Text of the main navigation element that expands/collapses the sub nav.   
*/  
text: string;  
/**   
* Optional ARIA label to describe the main navigation element.   
*/  
ariaLabel?: string;  
/**   
* Indicates if the sub navigation links are expanded or collapsed.   
*/  
/** 
* Manages the expanded and active state of a single sub-navigation link. 
* @returns props to set on the `SidebarNavSubLinks` component. 
*/
export const useSingleNavSubLinks = (): IUseSingleNavSubLinksReturn => {  
const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<boolean>(false);  
const push = useNavigate();
  // Automatically visit the first sublink if the user expands and it's not active yet  
const onChangeExpand = (props: IOnChangeProps) => {    
const { active, expanded, subLinks } = props;    
setExpanded(expanded);    
if (expanded && !active) {      
const [{ route: firstRoute }] = subLinks;      
push(firstRoute);    
}  
};  
// Automatically expand/collapse the section when its active state changes  
const onChangeActive = (props: IOnChangeProps) => {    
const { active, expanded } = props;    
if (expanded !== active) {      
setExpanded(active);    
}  
};
  return {    
if (expanded !== active) {      
setExpanded(active);    
}  
};
  return {    
expanded,    
onChangeExpand,    
onChangeActive,  
};
};
```

transform: rotateZ(0deg);

transform: rotateZ(360deg);

transform: rotate(0);

0%,  
25% {    
stroke-dashoffset: 280;    
transform: rotate(0);

50%,  
75% {    
stroke-dashoffset: 75;    
transform: rotate(45deg);

100% {    
stroke-dashoffset: 280;    
transform: rotate(360deg);  
}

opacity: 0;  
}

opacity: 1;  
`;
export const SCSpinner = styled.div`

animation: ${fadeIn} 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;

animation: 2s linear infinite ${svgAnimation};    
max-width: 100px;
    & circle {      
animation: 1.4s ease-in-out infinite both ${circleAnimation};      
display: block;      
fill: transparent;

}  
}

* @param value input field value 
* @param onChange 
* @param placeholder input field placeholder text 
* @param maskToggle if true, allows toggling the visibility of the SSN 
*/
const SSNInput = ({  
id,  
name,  
value = '',  
onChange,  
className,  
disabled,  
maskToggle = false,  
...rest
}: ISSNInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();  
const maskedValue = applyInputMask(value, MASK_FORMAT.SSN);  
const visibleMaskedValue = maskedValue.slice(0, 7).replace(/\d/g, '*').concat(maskedValue.slice(7));  
const [showSSN, setShowSSN] = useState(false);
  return (    
<SCSSNTextFieldWrapper>      
<SCMaskedInput        
id={id}        
name={name}        
name={name}        
fieldType={FieldTypeValues.SSN}        
value={maskedValue}        
onChange={onChange}        
className={className}        
disabled={disabled}        
autoComplete='off'        
{...rest}      
/>
      <SCVisibleValueWrap className={className} disabled={disabled}>        
<SCInvisibleValue>          
{/* Transparent value. Used to get the SCMaskedValue width */}          
{maskedValue}
          <SCMaskedValue>            
{showSSN && maskToggle ? (              
<span>{maskedValue}</span>            
) : (              
<>                
{/* Masked first 5 digits and 2 dashes */}                
<SCMaskedValueStars>                  
{visibleMaskedValue                    
.slice(0, 7)                    
.split('')                    
.map((char: string, idx: number) => (                      
.split('')                    
.map((char: string, idx: number) => (                      
<span key={idx}>{char}</span>                    
))}                
</SCMaskedValueStars>                
{/* Visible last 4 digits */}                
<span>{visibleMaskedValue.slice(7)}</span>              
</>            
)}          
</SCMaskedValue>        
</SCInvisibleValue>        
{maskToggle && !disabled && (          
<SCToggleMask            
role='button'            
aria-label={showSSN ? t('INPUT__HIDE_SSN') : t('INPUT__SHOW_SSN')}            
tabIndex={0}            
onClick={(): void => {              
setShowSSN && setShowSSN(!showSSN);            
}}            
onKeyDown={(event): void => {              
if (a11yKeydown(event)) {                
setShowSSN && setShowSSN(!showSSN);              
}            
}}>            
}}            
onKeyDown={(event): void => {              
if (a11yKeydown(event)) {                
setShowSSN && setShowSSN(!showSSN);              
}            
}}>            
{showSSN ? <Icon name={ICON_TYPES.EYE_CROSSED} size={18} /> : <Icon name={ICON_TYPES.EYE} size={18} />}          
</SCToggleMask>        
)}      
</SCVisibleValueWrap>    
</SCSSNTextFieldWrapper>  
);
};
SSNInput.displayName = 'SSNInput';
export { SSNInput };
```
