id,    
items: optionItems,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
});  
const selectedItem = options.find((option) => option.value === value) ?? null;
  return (    
<>      
{label && (        
<SCLabelWrap>          
<SCLabel {...getLabelProps()} className='StylizedDropdown__label'>            
{label}          
</SCLabel>          
{labelIcon}        
</SCLabelWrap>      
{labelIcon}        
</SCLabelWrap>      
)}      
{ariaLabel && !label && <SCSROnlyLabel {...getLabelProps()}>{ariaLabel}</SCSROnlyLabel>}
      <SCStylizedDropdown id={id} className={`StylizedDropdown ${className} ${isOpen ? 'isOpen' : ''}`}>        
<SCTrigger {...getToggleButtonProps({ className: 'StylizedDropdown__trigger' })}>          
{triggerElement}          
{!!triggerIcon && <SCTriggerIcon>{triggerIcon}</SCTriggerIcon>}        
</SCTrigger>        
<SCMenu          
{...getMenuProps({            
className: 'StylizedDropdown__menu',          
})}>          
{isOpen && (            
<>              
{optionItems.map((item, index) => (                
<SCMenuItem                  
key={item.value}                  
{...getItemProps({                    
item,                    
index,                    
className: `StylizedDropdown__menu-item ${highlightedIndex === index ? 'highlighted' : ''} ${                      
selectedItem === item ? 'selected' : ''                    
} ${item.disabled ? 'disabled' : ''}${item.value === OPTION_BUTTON_VALUE ? 'option-button' : ''}`,                  
})}>                  
{item.jsxLabel ? item.jsxLabel : item.label}                  
{selectedItem === item && (                    
<SCPaddedIcon>                      
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} />                    
</SCPaddedIcon>                  
)}                
</SCMenuItem>              
))}            
</>          
)}        
</SCMenu>      
</SCStylizedDropdown>    
</>  
);
};
export { StylizedDropdown };

import React, { useState, useEffect, useContext, SyntheticEvent, KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import { ColorModeContext } from 'styles/modes';
import { SCTabList, SCTab } from './styles';
import { ITabsProps } from './types';
export const isNode = (node: HTMLElement): boolean => node && 'getAttribute' in node;
export const isRadioButtonNode = (node: HTMLElement): boolean => isNode(node) && node.getAttribute('role') === 'radio';
const getIndexByLabel = (value: string, labels: { label: string; value: string }[]): number => {  
const index = labels.findIndex((l) => l.value === value) || 0;  
return index > -1 ? index : 0;
};
const getLabelByIndex = (index: number, data: { label: string; value: string }[]): string => data[index].value || '';
const getIndexByLabel = (value: string, labels: { label: string; value: string }[]): number => {  
const index = labels.findIndex((l) => l.value === value) || 0;  
return index > -1 ? index : 0;
};
const getLabelByIndex = (index: number, data: { label: string; value: string }[]): string => data[index].value || '';
/** 
* Basic binary toggle switch component. 
* @requires ColorModeContext 
*/
const Tabs = ({ id, className, data, onSelect, disabledValue, defaultChecked, label }: ITabsProps): JSX.Element => {  
const [index, setIndex] = useState(getIndexByLabel(defaultChecked, data));  
const mode = useContext(ColorModeContext);
  useEffect(() => {    
setIndex(getIndexByLabel(defaultChecked, data));  
}, [defaultChecked, data]);
  const onSelectHandler = (e: SyntheticEvent): void => {    
const node = e.target as HTMLElement;
    if (!isRadioButtonNode(node) || !node.parentNode) return;
    const arr: HTMLElement[] = [];    
const newIndex = arr.slice.call(node.parentNode.children).indexOf(node);    
const label = getLabelByIndex(newIndex, data);
    onSelect && onSelect(label);    
setIndex(() => newIndex);  
};
    if (!isRadioButtonNode(node) || !node.parentNode) return;
    const arr: HTMLElement[] = [];    
const newIndex = arr.slice.call(node.parentNode.children).indexOf(node);    
const label = getLabelByIndex(newIndex, data);
    onSelect && onSelect(label);    
setIndex(() => newIndex);  
};
  const onKeyDownHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent<HTMLElement>): void => {    
if (['Enter', ' '].includes(e.key)) {      
e.preventDefault();      
onSelectHandler(e);    
}  
};
  return (    
<SCTabList      
className={className}      
id={`${id}_radiogroup`}      
role='radiogroup'      
mode={mode}      
onClick={onSelectHandler}      
onKeyDown={onKeyDownHandler}      
aria-label={label}>      
{data.map((item, i) => {        
const isSelected = index === i;        
const isSelected = index === i;        
const isDisabled = disabledValue === item.value;
        return (          
<SCTab            
id={`${id}_radio-${i}`}            
key={`${item.label}-key` || i}            
value={item.value}            
className={`${className ? `${className} ` : ''}${index === i + 1 ? 'prev' : ''}`}            
aria-checked={isSelected}            
role='radio'            
aria-disabled={isDisabled}            
tabIndex={isDisabled ? -1 : 0}            
aria-label={item.label}>            
{item.label}          
</SCTab>        
);      
})}    
</SCTabList>  
);
};
export { Tabs };

### 444. `C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\atoms\ToggleButton\ToggleButton.tsx`

```typescript
import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { ColorModeContext } from 'styles/modes';
import { SCToggleWrapper, SCToggle } from './styles';
import { IToggleButtonProps, ToggleSize } from './types';
import { a11yKeydown } from '../a11y';
import { IToggleButtonProps, ToggleSize } from './types';
import { a11yKeydown } from '../a11y';
/** 
* Basic binary toggle switch component.  Should be read as a Radio button from ADA perspective. 
* @requires ColorModeContext 
*/
const ToggleButton = ({  
id = '',  
options,  
selectedValue,  
isDisabled = false,  
size = ToggleSize.SMALL,  
onChange,  
className,
}: IToggleButtonProps): JSX.Element => {  
const mode = useContext(ColorModeContext);
  return (    
<SCToggleWrapper      
id={`${id}_radiogroup`}      
role='radiogroup'      
mode={mode}      
size={size}      
isDisabled={isDisabled}      
className={className}>      
className={className}>      
{options.map((item, i) => {        
const isSelected = item.value === selectedValue;
        return (          
<SCToggle            
id={`${id}_toggle_radio-${i}`}            
key={`${item.label}-toggle-key-${i}` || i}            
value={item.value}            
isSelected={isSelected}            
isSecondBtn={i === 1}            
isDisabled={isDisabled}            
size={size}            
aria-label={item.ariaLabel ?? item.label}            
aria-checked={isSelected}            
aria-disabled={isDisabled}            
tabIndex={isDisabled ? -1 : 0}            
mode={mode}            
role='radio'            
onClick={() => onChange(item.label, item.value)}            
onKeyDown={(e: KeyboardEvent | React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLLIElement>) => {              
if (a11yKeydown(e)) {                
onChange(item.label, item.value);              
}            
}}>            
}}>            
{item.label}          
</SCToggle>        
);      
})}    
</SCToggleWrapper>  
);
};
export { ToggleButton };
```

### 448. `C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\atoms\Tooltip\Tooltip.tsx`

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { SCTooltip, SCTooltipTitle, SCTooltipContent, SCTooltipFooter, SCTooltipLink, SCTooltipBtn } from './styles';
import { TooltipDirection, ITooltipProps, TooltipSize } from './types';
const noop = () => {};
/** 
* Tooltip 
* @summary 
* Tooltip info taken from Figma 
* Tooltip 
* @summary 
* Tooltip info taken from Figma 
* The tooltip will vertically grow as more text is added to it 
* The position the tooltip can be moved using the class tooltip__override 
* example & .tooltip__override { top: 25px; left: -114px; } 
*/
const Tooltip = ({  
id,  
className = '',  
title,  
titleAlign,  
body,  
linkText,  
linkUrl = '',  
btnText,  
direction = TooltipDirection.BOTTOM_LEFT,  
size = TooltipSize.SMALL,  
onClick = noop,
}: ITooltipProps): JSX.Element => {  
const componentId = id ? `tooltip-${id}` : undefined;
  return (    
<SCTooltip id={componentId} className={`${className} tooltip__override`} direction={direction} size={size}>      
{title && <SCTooltipTitle align={titleAlign}>{title}</SCTooltipTitle>}      
{title && <SCTooltipTitle align={titleAlign}>{title}</SCTooltipTitle>}      
{body && <SCTooltipContent size={size}>{body}</SCTooltipContent>}      
{size === TooltipSize.LARGE && (        
<SCTooltipFooter>          
{linkText && <SCTooltipLink href={linkUrl}>{linkText}</SCTooltipLink>}          
{btnText && (            
<SCTooltipBtn size='small' onClick={onClick}>              
{btnText}            
</SCTooltipBtn>          
)}        
</SCTooltipFooter>      
)}    
</SCTooltip>  
);
};
export { Tooltip };
```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
enum Key {  
ENTER_KEY = 'Enter',  
SPACE_KEY = ' ',  
SPACE_KEY_ALT = 'Space',
}
/** 
* Determines appropriate keydowns ('space' and 'enter') for accessible interactions 
* and returns true or false 
* @param e 
*/
export function a11yKeydown(  
/** 
* Determines appropriate keydowns ('space' and 'enter') for accessible interactions 
* and returns true or false 
* @param e 
*/
export function a11yKeydown(  
e:    
| KeyboardEvent    
| React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLLIElement>    
| React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>    
| React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>
): boolean | undefined {  
if (e.type === 'keydown') {    
const { key } = e;    
if (key === Key.ENTER_KEY || key === Key.SPACE_KEY || key === Key.SPACE_KEY_ALT) {      
return true;    
}  
}  
return false;
}
```

import React from 'react';
import { formatDatetime } from '../../../helpers/formatDate';
import {  
SCActivityFeedWrapper,  
SCItemMarker,  
SCActivityListItemText,  
SCActivityListItemTitle,  
SCLongLine,  
SCProgressList,  
SCActivityMarker,  
SCActivityListItemDescription,
} from './styles';
import { IActivityProps, IActivity } from './types';
import { useTranslation } from 'react-i18next';
import { Markdown } from '../Markdown';
/** 
* Renders an activity list 
* @param {string} activities - List of activities to be rendered 
*/
*/
export const ActivityFeed = ({ activities }: IActivityProps) => {  
const { t } = useTranslation();  
const pItems = (    
<>      
{activities.map((singleActivity: IActivity) => {        
return (          
<SCActivityFeedWrapper            
data-testid={`activity_${singleActivity.id}`}            
aria-label={`activity_${singleActivity.id}`}            
role='img'            
id={`activity_${singleActivity.id}`}            
key={`activity_${singleActivity.id}`}>            
<SCItemMarker id='progressListItemMarker'>              
<SCActivityMarker                
activityOrigin={singleActivity.originID}                
aria-label={t('ACTIVITY_MARKER_ARIA')}                
role='img'              
/>              
<SCLongLine />            
</SCItemMarker>            
<SCActivityListItemText>              
<SCActivityListItemTitle>                
<Markdown text={singleActivity.formattedString} />              
</SCActivityListItemTitle>              
<SCActivityListItemDescription>                
{formatDatetime(singleActivity.dateTime, false, false, true)}              
</SCActivityListItemDescription>            
</SCActivityListItemText>          
</SCActivityFeedWrapper>        
);      
})}    
</>  
);
  return (    
<>      
<SCProgressList>{pItems}</SCProgressList>    
</>  
);
};
```

import React from 'react';
import actionCreatorFactory from 'typescript-fsa';
import { IAddress } from 'core/types';
import { IAddressVerifyState } from './types';
export const actionCreator = actionCreatorFactory('ADDRESS');
export const verifyAddress = actionCreator.async<  
void,  
{ address: IAddress; verifiedAddress?: IAddress },  
{ message: string; address: IAddress }
>('VERIFY_ADDRESS');
export const setSelectedAddress = actionCreator<string>('SELECTED_ADDRESS');
export const setModal = actionCreator<boolean>('SET_MODAL');
export const setUpdateAddress = actionCreator<boolean>('SET_UPDATE');
export const resetState = actionCreator<IAddressVerifyState>('RESET_STATE');
>('VERIFY_ADDRESS');
export const setSelectedAddress = actionCreator<string>('SELECTED_ADDRESS');
export const setModal = actionCreator<boolean>('SET_MODAL');
export const setUpdateAddress = actionCreator<boolean>('SET_UPDATE');
export const resetState = actionCreator<IAddressVerifyState>('RESET_STATE');
/** 
* This thunk goes and calls to grab and application based off of the loanAppId. 
* Also takes a signal so the call can be aborted. 
* 
* @param ICallGetApplication 
*/
export const callVerifyAddress = (  
address: IAddress,  
verifyAddressAPI: (address: IAddress) => Promise<IAddress | undefined>
): ((dispatch: React.Dispatch<any>) => Promise<void>) => {  
return async (dispatch: React.Dispatch<any>): Promise<void> => {    
try {      
dispatch(verifyAddress.started());      
const verifiedAddress = await verifyAddressAPI(address);      
dispatch(        
verifyAddress.done({          
result: { verifiedAddress: verifiedAddress as IAddress, address },        
})      
);    
} catch (err) {      
// In case the call fails don't block the user      
dispatch(        
verifyAddress.failed({          
error: {            
message: err.message,            
address,          
},        
})      
);    
}  
};
};
```

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';

```typescript
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { Modal } from 'components/molecules/Modal';
import { CoreButton } from 'components/atoms/CoreButton';
import { RadioGroup } from 'components/atoms';
import { IModalProps } from 'store/modal';
import {  
SCModalTitle,  
SCModalContent,  
SCTextParagraph,  
SCModalContentWrapper,  
SCFooterWrapper,  
SCAddressLabel,  
SCModalSubTitle,  
SCTextAddressNotFoundheader,  
SCAppModalFooter,  
SCAddressText,
} from './styles';
import { useHandleState } from './handlers';
import { Alignment, Size } from 'core/enums';
/** 
* AddressVerifyModal text content wrapped inside a `ModalContainer`. 
* @requires APIContext.verifyAddress 
* AddressVerifyModal text content wrapped inside a `ModalContainer`. 
* @requires APIContext.verifyAddress 
*/
const AddressVerifyModal = ({ payload, onClose, onContinue }: IModalProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { onSelectAddress, addressData, isAddressFound, modalOpen, updateAddressOnClick, selectedIndex } =    
useHandleState({ payload, onClose, onContinue });
  const { t } = useTypedTranslation();  
const mainHeaderForAddress = t('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__SUB_HEADER_TITLE');  
const mainHeaderForNotFoundAddress = t('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__SUB_HEADER_NOT_FOUND');  
const subHeaderForAddress = t('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__HELP_TEXT');  
const subHeaderForNotFoundAddress = t('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__HELP_TEXT_NOT_FOUND');  
const topHeader = t('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__HEADER_TITLE');  
const isVerifiedAddress = !!isAddressFound && Array.isArray(addressData) && selectedIndex === 'address_0';  
const handleContinueClick = () => {    
if (payload?.handleButtonClick) {      
// track what option user selected when continue button is clicked      
payload.handleButtonClick(payload.groupCode, 'addressSelection', isVerifiedAddress);    
}    
updateAddressOnClick();  
};  
const handleCloseClick = () => {    
if (payload?.handleButtonClick) {      
payload.handleButtonClick(payload.groupCode, 'closedAddressModal');    
}    
payload.handleButtonClick(payload.groupCode, 'closedAddressModal');    
}    
onClose?.();  
};
  // track when modal is shown to user and if suggested address is found  
useEffect(() => {    
if (modalOpen && payload?.handleButtonClick) {      
if (isAddressFound && Array.isArray(addressData)) {        
payload.handleButtonClick(payload.groupCode, 'foundAddress');      
} else {        
payload.handleButtonClick(payload.groupCode, 'noAddress');      
}    
}  
}, [modalOpen]);
  return (    
<>      
{modalOpen && (        
<Modal          
close={handleCloseClick}          
hidePadding          
ariaTitle=''          
title={topHeader}          
footer={            
<SCFooterWrapper>              
<SCAppModalFooter id='verify-address-modal-footer' stacked>                
<CoreButton onClick={handleCloseClick} id='verify-address-cancel' isSecondary>                  
{t('GLOBAL__BACK').toString()}                
</CoreButton>                
<CoreButton onClick={handleContinueClick} id='verify-address-continue'>                  
{t('GLOBAL__CONTINUE').toString()}                
</CoreButton>              
</SCAppModalFooter>            
</SCFooterWrapper>          
}>          
<>            
<SCModalTitle id='dialog-heading'>              
{isAddressFound ? mainHeaderForAddress : mainHeaderForNotFoundAddress}            
</SCModalTitle>            
<SCModalSubTitle id='dialog-subheading'>              
{isAddressFound ? subHeaderForAddress : subHeaderForNotFoundAddress}            
</SCModalSubTitle>            
<SCModalContent id='dialog-content'>              
{isAddressFound && Array.isArray(addressData) ? (                
<RadioGroup                  
name='address'                  
options={addressData.map(({ address1, address2, label }, index) => ({                    
value: `address_${index}`,                    
label: <SCAddressLabel> {label}</SCAddressLabel>,                    
labelDescription: (                      
<>                        
<SCAddressText>{`${address1}`}</SCAddressText>                        
<SCAddressText>{` ${address2}`}</SCAddressText>                      
</>                    
),                  
}))}                  
value={selectedIndex}                  
alignment={Alignment.VERTICAL}                  
radioSize={Size.SMALL}                  
onChange={onSelectAddress}                
/>              
) : (                
<SCModalContentWrapper>                  
<SCTextAddressNotFoundheader> {addressData[0].label} </SCTextAddressNotFoundheader>                  
<SCTextParagraph>{`${addressData[0].address1}`}</SCTextParagraph>                  
<SCTextParagraph>{` ${addressData[0].address2}`}</SCTextParagraph>                
</SCModalContentWrapper>              
)}            
</SCModalContent>          
</>        
</Modal>      
)}    
</>  
);
</SCModalContent>          
</>        
</Modal>      
)}    
</>  
);
};
export { AddressVerifyModal };
```

### 459. `C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\molecules\AddressVerifyModal\addressVerifyReducer.ts`
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\CommonUI\src\components\molecules\AddressVerifyModal\addressVerifyReducer.ts" type=".ts" size="2963" lines="89" chars="2963" -->
```typescript
import { reducerWithoutInitialState } from 'typescript-fsa-reducers';
import { isEqual } from 'lodash';
import { setSelectedAddress, setUpdateAddress, setModal, verifyAddress, resetState } from './addressVerifyActions';
import { formatAddress, removeEmptyPropsFromAddress } from './helpers';
import { IAddressVerifyState } from './types';
import { IAddress } from 'core/types';
export const addressVerifyReducer = reducerWithoutInitialState<IAddressVerifyState>()  
.case(verifyAddress.done, (state, payload) => {    
.case(verifyAddress.done, (state, payload) => {    
const { groupTitle, addressStateReadOnly } = state;    
const { verifiedAddress, address } = payload.result;    
const addressStateMatches = !!address.state && address.state === verifiedAddress?.state;    
if (verifiedAddress) {      
const updatedAddress = removeEmptyPropsFromAddress({ ...address });      
const cleanedAddress = removeEmptyPropsFromAddress({        
...verifiedAddress,      
});      
const isItVerifiedAddress = isEqual(cleanedAddress, updatedAddress);      
if (isItVerifiedAddress) {        
return {          
...state,          
selectedAddress: address,          
updateAddress: true,        
};      
}      
// skip adding verifiedAddress as an option if state data field is disabled and state values differ      
if (!addressStateReadOnly || (addressStateReadOnly && addressStateMatches)) {        
const updatedState = {          
addressData: [            
formatAddress(verifiedAddress as IAddress, groupTitle, true),            
formatAddress(address, groupTitle),          
],          
addressList: [verifiedAddress, address],          
selectedAddress: verifiedAddress,          
selectedIndex: 'address_0',          
modalOpen: true,          
isAddressFound: true,        
};        
return {          
...state,          
...updatedState,        
};      
}    
}    
return {      
...state,      
addressData: [formatAddress(address, groupTitle)],      
addressList: [address],      
modalOpen: true,      
isAddressFound: false,    
};  
})  
.case(verifyAddress.failed, (state, payload) => {    
const { groupTitle } = state;    
const { address } = payload.error;    
return {      
...state,      
addressData: [formatAddress(address, groupTitle)],      
addressList: [address],      
modalOpen: true,      
selectedAddress: address,      
isAddressFound: false,    
};  
})  
.case(setSelectedAddress, (state, payload) => {    
const index = Number(payload.split('_')[1]); // not a fan of this    
return {      
...state,      
selectedIndex: payload,      
selectedAddress: state.addressList[index],    
};  
})  
.case(setUpdateAddress, (state, payload) => {    
return {      
...state,      
updateAddress: payload,    
};  
})  
.case(setModal, (state, payload) => {    
return {      
...state,      
})  
.case(setModal, (state, payload) => {    
return {      
...state,      
modalOpen: payload,    
};  
})  
.case(resetState, (state, payload) => {    
return payload;  
});
```

import { IDisplayAddress, IDynamicAddress } from './types';
import { IAddress } from 'core/types';
import { nonHookTypedTranslation, Translator } from 'i18n';
export const getAddressLabel = (groupTitle: string, isVerify?: boolean) => {  
const prefix = isVerify ? 'Verified' : 'Entered';  
if (groupTitle.toLocaleLowerCase().includes('home')) {    
return nonHookTypedTranslation('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__ADDRESS_LABEL', { prefix, type: 'home' });  
}  
if (groupTitle.toLocaleLowerCase().includes('mail')) {    
return nonHookTypedTranslation('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__ADDRESS_LABEL', { prefix, type: 'mailing' });  
}  
return nonHookTypedTranslation('ADDRESS_VERIFY_MODAL__ADDRESS_LABEL_GENERIC', { prefix });
};
export const formatAddress = (address: IAddress, groupTitle: string, isVerify?: boolean): IDisplayAddress => {  
const { address1, address2, city, state, zip5, zip4 } = address;  
return {    
address1: `${address1 || ''}${address2 ? `, ${address2}` : ''}`,    
address2: `${city}, ${state}, ${zip5} ${zip4 ? `-${zip4}` : ''}`,    
label: getAddressLabel(groupTitle, isVerify),  
};
address1: `${address1 || ''}${address2 ? `, ${address2}` : ''}`,    
address2: `${city}, ${state}, ${zip5} ${zip4 ? `-${zip4}` : ''}`,    
label: getAddressLabel(groupTitle, isVerify),  
};
};
export const removeEmptyPropsFromAddress = (address: IDynamicAddress): IDynamicAddress => {  
Object.keys(address).forEach(    
// eslint-disable-next-line    
(key: string) => (address[key] === '' || address[key] === undefined) && delete address[key]  
);  
return address;
};
export const getInitialState = (t: Translator, groupTitle: string, addressStateReadOnly: boolean) => {  
return {    
t,    
addressData: [],    
addressList: [],    
updateAddress: false,    
groupTitle,    
addressStateReadOnly,  
};
};

import React from 'react';
import { IApplicationTimeInfoSectionProps } from './types';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { Markdown } from 'components/molecules/Markdown';
import { TimeToComplete } from 'components/atoms/TimeToComplete';
import {  
SCPageInfoWrapper,  
SCTimeToCompleteWrapper,  
SCPageInfoText,  
SCListHeading,  
SCList,  
SCListItem,  
SCMarkdown,
SCMarkdown,
} from './styles';
/** 
* PageInfo 
* @summary 
* To be used as 'infoChildren' prop in MortgageGetStarted to display additional information 
* @param listHeadingText 
* Translation key (text can include markdown) for info text above list items 
* @param listHeadingTranslationOptions 
* Translation options for listHeadingText 
* @param timeToApply 
* Number in minutes to complete application 
* @param minTimeToApply 
* @param maxTimeToApply 
* Numbers in minutes to complete application 
* @param minTimeToProcess 
* @param maxTimeToProcess 
* Numbers in days to process application 
* @param timeToApplyText 
* @param timeToProcessText 
* Translation keys that are displayed after timeframe 
* @param shouldKnowList 
* An array of translation keys that are translated into list items in an unordered list 
* @param shouldKnowListTranslated 
* @param shouldKnowListTranslated 
* An array of strings that become list items in an unordered list 
* @param showTimeSection 
* A boolean for showing/hiding the time section 
* @param showInfoSection 
* A boolean for showing/hiding the info section 
*/
const ApplicationTimeInfoSection = ({  
listHeadingText,  
listHeadingTranslationOptions,  
timeToApply,  
minTimeToApply,  
maxTimeToApply,  
timeToApplyText,  
minTimeToProcess,  
maxTimeToProcess,  
timeToProcess,  
timeToProcessText,  
shouldKnowList,  
shouldKnowListTranslated,  
showTimeSection = true,  
showInfoSection = true,
}: IApplicationTimeInfoSectionProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
  return (    
<SCPageInfoWrapper>      
{showTimeSection && (        
<SCTimeToCompleteWrapper>          
{minTimeToApply && maxTimeToApply && (            
<TimeToComplete              
id='time-to-apply'              
ariaLabel={`${minTimeToApply} ${t('GLOBAL__TO')} ${maxTimeToApply} ${t('MINUTES_TO_APPLY')}`}              
displayText={`${minTimeToApply}-${maxTimeToApply} ${t(timeToApplyText)}`}            
/>          
)}          
{timeToApply && (            
<TimeToComplete              
id='time-to-apply'              
ariaLabel={`${timeToApply} ${t('MINUTES_TO_APPLY')}`}              
displayText={`${timeToApply} ${t(timeToApplyText)}`}            
/>          
)}          
{minTimeToProcess && maxTimeToProcess && timeToProcessText && (            
<TimeToComplete              
id='time-to-process'              
ariaLabel={`${minTimeToProcess} ${t('GLOBAL__TO')} ${maxTimeToProcess} ${timeToProcessText}`}              
displayText={`${minTimeToProcess}-${maxTimeToProcess} ${t(timeToProcessText)}`}              
icon='calendar'            
/>          
)}          
{timeToProcess && timeToProcessText && (            
<TimeToComplete              
id='time-to-process'              
ariaLabel={`${timeToProcess} ${timeToProcessText}`}              
displayText={`${timeToProcess} ${t(timeToProcessText)}`}              
icon='calendar'            
/>          
)}        
</SCTimeToCompleteWrapper>      
)}      
{showInfoSection && (        
<SCPageInfoText>          
<SCListHeading id='application_time_list_heading'>            
<Markdown text={t(listHeadingText, listHeadingTranslationOptions)} />          
</SCListHeading>          
<SCList id='application_time_display_list'>            
{shouldKnowList?.map((item) => (              
<SCListItem key={item} id={`display_${item}`}>                
<SCMarkdown text={t(item)} />              
</SCListItem>            
))}            
{shouldKnowListTranslated?.map((item) => (              
))}            
{shouldKnowListTranslated?.map((item) => (

</SCListItem>            
))}          
</SCList>        
</SCPageInfoText>      
)}    
</SCPageInfoWrapper>  
);
};
export { ApplicationTimeInfoSection };

export const formatValueAsJsxOrMarkdown = (value?: string | string[] | JSX.Element): JSX.Element | undefined => {  
if (typeof value === 'string' || Array.isArray(value)) {    
return <Markdown text={value} />;  
}
  return value;
};
export const AppModal = ({  
id = 'simple-confirm',  
body,  
title,  
ariaTitle,  
body,  
title,  
ariaTitle,  
headerHasFocus,  
continueBtnText,  
continueBtnDisabled,  
cancelBtnText,  
cancelBtnClassName,  
continueBtnClassName,  
onContinue,  
onClose,  
onCloseBtnClick,  
onContinueBtnClick,  
onXBtnClick,  
fullPageModal,  
cancelBtnOrder,  
continueBtnOrder,  
ghostBtnOrder,  
onGhostBtnClick,  
ghostBtnText,  
modalWindowClassName,  
stacked,  
logo,  
width,  
displayMode,
}: IModalProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { modalState } = useContext(ModalContext);  
const handleContinueClick = (): void => {    
onContinueBtnClick?.(modalState);    
onContinue?.(modalState);  
};
  const handleCancelClick = (): void => {    
onCloseBtnClick?.();    
onClose?.();  
};
  const handleGhostButtonClick = (): void => {    
onClose?.();    
onGhostBtnClick?.();  
};
  const handleXButtonClick = (): void => {    
onClose?.();    
onXBtnClick?.();  
};
  // for cases when we want a single button with primary styles and cancel functionality (InfoModal)  
const singleButtonWithCancel = !!(continueBtnText && cancelBtnText);  
const footer = (    
<SCAppModalFooter id='app_modal_footer' stacked={stacked}>      
{cancelBtnText && (        
const singleButtonWithCancel = !!(continueBtnText && cancelBtnText);  
const footer = (    
<SCAppModalFooter id='app_modal_footer' stacked={stacked}>      
{cancelBtnText && (        
<CoreButton          
order={cancelBtnOrder}          
isSecondary={singleButtonWithCancel}          
onClick={handleCancelClick}          
id={`${id}-cancel`}          
className={cancelBtnClassName}>          
{cancelBtnText}        
</CoreButton>      
)}      
{continueBtnText && (        
<CoreButton          
order={continueBtnOrder}          
onClick={handleContinueClick}          
id={`${id}-continue`}          
disabled={continueBtnDisabled}          
displayMode={displayMode}          
className={continueBtnClassName}>          
{continueBtnText}        
</CoreButton>      
)}      
{/* third button style option for modals - https://www.figma.com/file/V0GwCpIRN7TGXQPsYLWbi3/00.10-%F0%9F%8C%8ECore-UI-Kit-Library?node-id=124%3A2021 */}      
{ghostBtnText && (        
<CoreButton order={ghostBtnOrder} isGhost onClick={handleGhostButtonClick} id={`${id}-ghost`}>          
{ghostBtnText}        
</CoreButton>      
)}    
</SCAppModalFooter>  
);  
return (    
<Modal      
ariaTitle={ariaTitle}      
headerHasFocus={headerHasFocus}      
hasTitle={!!title}      
title={        
<SCApplicationModalTitle id={`${id}-modal-header`}>          
<SCLogoContainer logo={logo}>            
{logo && <img alt='imageAlt' />}            
{formatValueAsJsxOrMarkdown(title)}          
</SCLogoContainer>        
</SCApplicationModalTitle>      
}      
testId={id}      
close={handleXButtonClick}      
modalWindowClassName={modalWindowClassName}      
hidePadding      
fullPageModal={fullPageModal}      
width={width}      
footer={footer}      
displayMode={displayMode}>      
<SCModalContent id='modal_content_wrapper'>{formatValueAsJsxOrMarkdown(body)}</SCModalContent>    
</Modal>  
);
};

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useConfirmModal } from 'hooks/modal';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { BlockerFunction, useBlocker } from 'react-router-dom';
import { IBeforeUnloadProps } from './types';
const BeforeUnload = ({  
nativeDialogue,  
formIsValid,  
formIsDirty,  
onSaveAndContinue,  
tenantLogo,  
modalOverrides = {},  
openModalOnRepeatedExitAttempts,  
shouldAlwaysBlock = false,  
onBlock = undefined,
}: IBeforeUnloadProps): JSX.Element => {  
const [confirmModalIsVisible, setConfirmModalIsVisible] = useState(false);  
const [lastLocation, setLastLocation] = useState<string | null>(null);  
const [confirmedNavigation, setConfirmedNavigation] = useState(false);  
const [backButtonSelected, setBackButtonSelected] = useState<boolean>();  
const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();  
const showConfirmModal = useConfirmModal();  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
  useEffect(() => {    
const handleLeavePage = (eventRef: BeforeUnloadEvent): string => {      
const confirmationMessage = 'required message (do not remove)';      
// eslint-disable-next-line      
eventRef.returnValue = confirmationMessage; // Gecko, Trident, Chrome 34+      
return confirmationMessage; // Gecko, WebKit, Chrome <34    
};
    if (nativeDialogue && formIsDirty) {      
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleLeavePage);    
}
    return (): void => {      
window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleLeavePage);    
};  
}, [nativeDialogue, formIsDirty]);
  const handleBlockedNavigation: BlockerFunction = ({ nextLocation, historyAction }): boolean => {    
const backButton = historyAction === 'POP';
    if (      
!confirmedNavigation &&      
typeof nextLocation !== 'undefined' &&      
(formIsDirty || backButton || shouldAlwaysBlock)    
) {      
setBackButtonSelected(backButton);      
typeof nextLocation !== 'undefined' &&      
(formIsDirty || backButton || shouldAlwaysBlock)    
) {      
setBackButtonSelected(backButton);      
setConfirmModalIsVisible(true);      
setLastLocation(nextLocation?.pathname);      
onBlock?.(nextLocation, historyAction);      
return true;    
}
    return false;  
};
  const blocker = useBlocker(handleBlockedNavigation);
  const onStay = (): void => {    
if (blocker.state === 'blocked') {      
blocker.reset();    
}    
if (!openModalOnRepeatedExitAttempts) {      
setConfirmedNavigation(true);    
}  
};
  const onLeave = (shouldSave: boolean) => (): void => {    
if (blocker.state === 'blocked') {      
blocker.proceed();    
}    
};
  const onLeave = (shouldSave: boolean) => (): void => {    
if (blocker.state === 'blocked') {      
blocker.proceed();    
}    
if (shouldSave) onSaveAndContinue('save');  
};
  useEffect(() => {    
const askUserConfirmation = async (): Promise<void> => {      
const validFormConfirmProps = {        
title: modalOverrides?.title,        
ariaTitle: modalOverrides?.ariaTitle,        
body: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__VALID_BODY', {          
returnObjects: true,        
}),        
continueBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__VALID_BUTTON_STAY'),        
cancelBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__VALID_BUTTON_LEAVE'),        
onCloseBtnClick: onLeave(true),        
onContinueBtnClick: onStay,        
onXBtnClick: onStay,        
...modalOverrides,      
};
      const invalidFormConfirmProps = {        
title: modalOverrides?.title,        
title: modalOverrides?.title,        
ariaTitle: modalOverrides?.ariaTitle,        
body:          
modalOverrides?.body ||          
t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__INVALID_BODY', {            
returnObjects: true,          
}),        
continueBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__INVALID_BUTTON_STAY'),        
cancelBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__INVALID_BUTTON_LEAVE'),        
onCloseBtnClick: onLeave(false),        
onContinueBtnClick: onStay,        
onXBtnClick: onStay,        
...modalOverrides,      
};
      const leavingFormConfirmProps = {        
title: modalOverrides?.title,        
ariaTitle: modalOverrides?.ariaTitle,        
logo: tenantLogo,        
body: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__LEAVING_BODY', {          
returnObjects: true,        
}),        
continueBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__LEAVING_BUTTON_STAY_IN_APPLICATION'),        
cancelBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__LEAVING_BUTTON_SAVE_AND_EXIT'),        
onCloseBtnClick: onLeave(true),        
cancelBtnText: t('BEFORE_UNLOAD_MODAL__LEAVING_BUTTON_SAVE_AND_EXIT'),        
onCloseBtnClick: onLeave(true),        
onContinueBtnClick: onStay,        
onXBtnClick: onStay,        
...modalOverrides,      
};
      setConfirmModalIsVisible(false);      
try {        
if (formIsDirty) {          
if (formIsValid && !backButtonSelected) {            
await showConfirmModal(validFormConfirmProps);            
setConfirmedNavigation(true);          
} else {            
await showConfirmModal(invalidFormConfirmProps);          
}        
} else {          
await showConfirmModal(leavingFormConfirmProps);        
}      
} catch (error) {        
// eslint-disable-next-line no-console        
console.log(error.message);      
}    
};
    if (confirmModalIsVisible) {      
}      
} catch (error) {        
// eslint-disable-next-line no-console        
console.log(error.message);      
}    
};
    if (confirmModalIsVisible) {      
askUserConfirmation();    
}  
}, [confirmModalIsVisible, lastLocation, formIsDirty, formIsValid, showConfirmModal, onSaveAndContinue, t]);
  return <></>;
};
export { BeforeUnload };

import { IUseModalProps } from 'store/modal';
import { Action, Location } from 'history';
export interface IBeforeUnloadProps {  
/**   
* @nativeDialogue that evaluates to :true: displays the native onbeforeunload dialog with default content on reload and tab or window close.   
* @nativeDialogue that evaluates to :false: disables onbeforeunload.   
* */  
nativeDialogue?: boolean;  
/**   
* @deprecated   
* @customDialogue that evaluates to :true: will display the custom dialog modal with interactions and content defined by the other conditionals.   
* @customDialogue that evaluates to :false: will not display the custom dialog modal.   
* */  
customDialogue?: boolean;  
/**   
* @formIsDirty Property passed to the component if :true: indicates that the form has been interacted with.   
* @formIsDirty Property passed to the component if :false: indicates that the form has not been interacted with.   
* @formIsDirty Property passed to the component if :false: indicates that the form has not been interacted with.   
* */  
formIsDirty: boolean;  
/**   
* @formIsValid Property passed to the component if :true: indicates that all form fields are valid and that the form can be saved.   
* @formIsValid Property passed to the component if :false: indicates that not all form fields are valid and that the form cannot be saved.   
* */  
formIsValid: boolean;  
/**   
* @onSaveAndContinue Function and data passed to the component, can be used to save form data from modal.   
* */  
onSaveAndContinue: (value?: string) => void;  
/**   
* @tenantLogo Tenant logo URL   
* */  
tenantLogo?: string;  
/**   
* @tenantName Tenant name   
* */  
tenantName?: string;  
/**   
* @modalOverrides if defined, these properties will override the properties supplied to the modals that BeforeUnload displays.   
*/  
modalOverrides?: Partial<IUseModalProps>;  
/**   
* @modalOverrides if defined, these properties will override the properties supplied to the modals that BeforeUnload displays.   
*/  
modalOverrides?: Partial<IUseModalProps>;  
/**   
* @openModalOnRepeatedExitAttempts If :true: will show a modal each time the user attempts to exit then dismisses the prompt.   
* @openModalOnRepeatedExitAttempts If :false: will only show the modal once.   
* */  
openModalOnRepeatedExitAttempts?: boolean;  
/**   
* @formIsValid If :true: indicates the modal should be shown regardless if the form is dirty or the back button has been selected.   
* @formIsValid If :false: indicates normal logic.   
* */  
shouldAlwaysBlock?: boolean;
  /**   
* @onBlock Function that is used when the modal shows up.   
*/  
onBlock?: (nextLocation: Location, historyAction: Action) => void;
}

import type { Chart, ChartData, ChartOptions, TooltipItem, Color, TooltipLabelStyle } from 'chart.js';
import { IReportModuleProps } from '../../../atoms/ReportModule/types';
import { defaultTheme } from 'styles';
export const donutDefaultOptions = (showPercentages?: boolean): ChartOptions<'doughnut'> => {  
const percentString = (amt: number, total: number): string => {    
return Math.round(100 * (amt / total)).toFixed(1);  
};
  const baseOptions: ChartOptions<'doughnut'> = {    
locale: 'und',    
maintainAspectRatio: false,    
responsive: true,    
cutout: '65%',    
animation: {      
animateRotate: false,      
animateScale: true,    
},    
radius: 118.5,    
layout: {      
padding: 0,    
},    
plugins: {      
tooltip: {        
callbacks: {          
labelColor: (toolTipItem: TooltipItem<'doughnut'>): TooltipLabelStyle => {            
const { datasets } = toolTipItem.chart.data;            
const bgColor = datasets[0].backgroundColor ? datasets[0].backgroundColor : defaultTheme.blue50;            
return {              
borderColor: defaultTheme.contrastWhite,              
borderWidth: 1,              
backgroundColor: (bgColor as Color[])[toolTipItem.dataIndex],            
};          
},          
label: (toolTipItem) => {            
let label: string;            
const dChart = toolTipItem.chart as Chart<'doughnut'>;            
const total = dChart.data.datasets[0].data.reduce((a: number, b: number) => a + b);            
const amt = toolTipItem.raw as number;            
const percent = percentString(amt, total);            
if (showPercentages) {              
label = `${toolTipItem.label} (${percent}%): ${toolTipItem.formattedValue}`;            
} else {              
label = `${toolTipItem.label}: ${toolTipItem.formattedValue}`;            
}            
return label;          
},        
},      
},      
legend: {        
display: true,        
position: 'bottom' as const,        
labels: {          
usePointStyle: true,          
pointStyle: 'circle' as const,          
boxWidth: 6,          
/* padding: 16, */          
font: {            
size: 12,          
},          
generateLabels(chart: Chart<'doughnut'>) {            
const { data } = chart;            
if (data.labels && data.labels?.length && data.datasets.length && chart.legend) {              
const {                
labels: { pointStyle },              
} = chart.legend.options;              
return data.labels?.map((label, i) => {                
const meta = chart.getDatasetMeta(0);                
const style = meta.controller.getStyle(i, true);                
const amt = chart.data.datasets[0].data[i];                
const total = data.datasets[0].data.reduce((a, b) => a + b);                
const percent = percentString(amt, total);                
return {                  
text: showPercentages ? `${label as string} (${percent}%)` : (label as string),                  
fillStyle: style.backgroundColor as string,                  
strokeStyle: defaultTheme.contrastGreyscale08,                  
lineWidth: 1,                  
pointStyle,                  
hidden: !chart.getDataVisibility(i),                  
datasetIndex: i,                  
textAlign: 'center',                
};              
});            
}            
return [];          
},        
},      
} as any,    
},  
};
  return baseOptions;
};
textAlign: 'center',                
};              
});            
}            
return [];          
},        
},      
} as any,    
},  
};
  return baseOptions;
};
export interface IDonutChartReportModuleProps extends IReportModuleProps {  
donutChartData: ChartData<'doughnut'>;  
donutChartColors?: string[];  
showPercentages?: boolean;
}
